You are doing an architectural research task — no code changes, just deep reading and reasoned conclusions. Report back in writing.

## The big picture

There are three sibling repos in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/`:

1. **`runpod-lifecycle/`** — a small Python package that wraps the RunPod API. Today it exposes pod-lifecycle primitives: `launch()`, `Pod.wait_ready()`, `Pod.exec_ssh()`, `Pod.open_ssh_client()`, `Pod.terminate()`, plus a CLI.
2. **`vibecomfy/`** — a ComfyUI-runtime tool. Inside it lives `scripts/runpod_runner.py` (~1.2k lines), which is a generic "ship a repo to a freshly-launched pod, run a script, pull artifacts back" harness — plus several callers (`runpod_validate.py`, `runpod_model_matrix.py`, `runpod_corpus_matrix.py`, `runpod_matrix_plan.py`, `runpod_matrix_remote.py`). There's also a thin CLI wrapper at `vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py`.
3. **`Astrid/`** — a file-based toolkit-of-executors-and-orchestrators (see its README, AGENTS.md, and especially `project.md`). The user wants RunPod to be available to *any* Astrid orchestrator, not just VibeComfy. Astrid has a pack convention at `astrid/packs/external/` — e.g. `external/vibecomfy/` declares `executor.yaml` + `run.py` + `STAGE.md`.

## The architectural question I want answered

Right now, the "shipping recipe" (`runpod_runner.py`) lives inside vibecomfy. The user's plan in `Astrid/project.md` is to expose RunPod as an Astrid-level capability — `external.runpod.{provision,run,teardown}` — that any orchestrator (e.g. `lora_train`, `scene_render`) can use, and which vibecomfy itself eventually *consumes* rather than owns. I've already suggested the cleanest move is: **push the generic parts of `runpod_runner.py` down into `runpod-lifecycle`, and have both vibecomfy and Astrid be peer consumers.** I want you to pressure-test that and figure out exactly where the boundaries should fall.

## What I want you to investigate

### Part A — VibeComfy side: what's *actually* generic vs vibecomfy-specific?

Read these files end-to-end:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_runner.py` (~1.2k lines, the main thing)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_validate.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_model_matrix.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_corpus_matrix.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_matrix_plan.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/scripts/runpod_matrix_remote.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/vibecomfy/commands/runpod.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/docs/runpod.md` and `docs/runpod_smoke.md` for intent

For each major piece in `runpod_runner.py` — `PodGuard`, signal handlers, `upload_dir`, `_upload_tarball`, `_build_upload_tarball`, `run_pod`, `run_pod_detached`, `_upload_remote_script`, `_download_artifacts`, `_build_artifact_manifest`, `_collect_outputs`, `_collect_run_metadata`, `_collect_watchdogs`, `_collect_remote_logs`, `_write_artifact_report`, `_print_detached_summary`, exclude sets, env-var conventions, REMOTE_ROOT — classify it as:

- **GENERIC** (belongs in `runpod-lifecycle`'s lifted runner)
- **VIBECOMFY-SPECIFIC** (stays in vibecomfy)
- **MIXED** (generic core, vibecomfy-specific defaults/format) — and say exactly where the split should fall

Watch especially for:
- Things that *look* generic but secretly assume the VibeComfy filesystem layout (`out/corpus_matrix/results.tsv`, `out/runs/*/watchdog.json`, `out/runs/*/metadata.json`)
- Things that look vibecomfy-specific but are really just configurable defaults (REMOTE_ROOT, the exclude set, env-var names)
- What the watchdog/timeout semantics need from the caller
- How signal handling interacts with being called from a long-running parent process (Astrid orchestrator)
- The detached-poll machinery — is the poll-loop generic, or is what it parses out of stdout vibecomfy-specific?

### Part B — runpod-lifecycle side: what surface does it need to grow?

Read:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/{__init__,api,cli,config,discovery,errors,events,lifecycle,pod,ssh,storage}.py`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/runpod-lifecycle/README.md`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/runpod-lifecycle/VibeComfy2.md` if it exists
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/runpod-lifecycle/pyproject.toml`

Answer:
1. Does `Pod` already support **stateless reattach by pod_id**? (i.e. can you reconstruct a usable `Pod` object given only the id, without holding the launch coroutine's state in memory?) If not, what would it take to add? This is critical because Astrid executors are separate processes — a `provision` executor exits, then later an `exec` executor needs to talk to the same pod.
2. Is there already an events/streaming API (`events.py`)? Could the upload-progress heartbeats and detached-poll output piggyback on it, or is it a different concern?
3. What does the `config.py` / `RunPodConfig.from_env` surface look like? Does the env-var convention already accept consumer prefixes, or is it baked to one prefix?
4. Look at `cli.py` — what commands exist? If we lift the runner, should the lifecycle CLI grow `run` / `ship` / `exec` subcommands, or is that out of scope?

### Part C — Astrid side: what does the pack shape need to be?

Read:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/project.md` (the plan)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/AGENTS.md` and `README.md`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/astrid/packs/external/vibecomfy/{executor.yaml,run.py,STAGE.md}` — full pattern example
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/astrid/packs/external/fal_foley/` and `moirae/` — for variation in patterns
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/Astrid/astrid/packs/external/pack.yaml`
- The orchestrator/executor contracts: glance at `astrid/core/executor/`, `astrid/core/orchestrator/`, `astrid/contracts/`, `astrid/verify/` — enough to understand how artifacts flow between steps (`consumes`/`provides`), how `produces` are gate-verified, and how `repeat.until` / `nested` work
- `astrid/docs/creating-tools.md` if present
- `astrid/docs/templates/{executor,orchestrator,element}/` if present

Answer:
1. How do separate executor steps share state in Astrid? (artifact files only? a pod-handle JSON artifact is the right shape?)
2. What does the gate/verify layer expect — does the runner's existing `manifest.json` shape line up, or does Astrid have a different schema?
3. For the `provision → exec → teardown` pattern: does Astrid have an existing idiom for "guaranteed teardown even on failure" (try/finally at the orchestrator level)? Or does that have to live inside a composite executor?
4. Should there be one pack with three sibling executors (like `vibecomfy.run` + `vibecomfy.validate` in one `executor.yaml`), or three folders? Check how existing multi-executor packs are laid out.
5. Where do long-running detached jobs fit? Astrid likely assumes executors are subprocesses that return — does the detached-poll case need an orchestrator-level pattern instead?
6. **The watchdog problem**: the in-process max-runtime watchdog in PodGuard dies when the `provision` executor exits. What's the right Astrid-idiomatic replacement? (e.g. a `astrid doctor` sweep that finds stale pod_handle.json files past their `terminate_at` and force-terminates? a separate long-lived watchdog daemon? a flag that says "the calling orchestrator owns the timer"?)
7. Does Astrid already have any concept of remote/sandboxed execution, or is this the first one? Search for it.

### Part D — Synthesis

Pull it together into a written architectural recommendation:

1. **Three-layer diagram in prose**: what's in `runpod-lifecycle` (post-lift), what's in `vibecomfy` (post-shrink), what's in `Astrid/packs/external/runpod/`. Be concrete — name the modules/files.
2. **The lifecycle public API** after the lift: what new functions/classes does `runpod-lifecycle` expose? Sketch the signatures.
3. **The Astrid pack shape**: file layout, executor ids, what each one's `consumes`/`provides` looks like, where the composite "one-shot ship & run" lives.
4. **The migration order**: what needs to ship first, what depends on what, what risks are there (multi-repo coordination, version pinning, backward compat for existing vibecomfy callers).
5. **Open questions / things you couldn't answer from reading** — call these out explicitly so I know what to come back to the user about.

170	    parts = Path(rel).parts
171	    return (
172	        any(rel == item or rel.startswith(f"{item}/") or item in parts for item in exclude_set)

177	def upload_dir(sftp, local: Path, remote: str, exclude_set: set[str], *, progress: UploadHeartbeat | None = None) -> None:
178	    try:
179	        sftp.mkdir(remote)
180	    except OSError:
181	        pass
182	    for child in local.iterdir():
183	        if should_skip(child, ROOT, exclude_set):
184	            continue
185	        remote_child = posixpath.join(remote, child.name)
186	        if child.is_dir():
187	            upload_dir(sftp, child, remote_child, exclude_set, progress=progress)
188	        else:
189	            size = child.stat().st_size
190	            if progress is not None:
191	                sent = 0
192	
193	                def _progress(current: int, _total: int) -> None:
194	                    nonlocal sent
195	                    delta = max(0, current - sent)
196	                    sent = current
197	                    progress.tick(bytes_added=delta)
198	
199	                sftp.put(str(child), remote_child, callback=_progress)
200	                progress.tick(files=1, bytes_added=max(0, size - sent))
201	            else:
202	                sftp.put(str(child), remote_child)

205	def install_signal_handlers(loop) -> asyncio.Event:
206	    cancel_event = asyncio.Event()
207	    task = asyncio.current_task(loop=loop)
208	    signal_count = 0
209	
210	    def _handle_signal(sig: signal.Signals) -> None:
211	        nonlocal signal_count
212	        signal_count += 1
213	        print(f"interrupt_requested signal={sig.name} cleanup=true", flush=True)
214	        cancel_event.set()
215	        if task is not None and not task.done():
216	            task.cancel()
217	        if signal_count > 1:
218	            print("interrupt_repeated=true cleanup_still_in_progress=true", flush=True)
219	
220	    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM):
221	        try:
222	            loop.add_signal_handler(sig, _handle_signal, sig)
223	        except (NotImplementedError, RuntimeError):
224	            previous = signal.getsignal(sig)
225	
226	            def _fallback(signum, frame, *, previous=previous) -> None:
227	                loop.call_soon_threadsafe(_handle_signal, signal.Signals(signum))
228	                if callable(previous) and previous not in {signal.SIG_DFL, signal.SIG_IGN}:
229	                    previous(signum, frame)
230	
231	            signal.signal(sig, _fallback)
232	    return cancel_event

236	    task = asyncio.current_task()
237	    if task is None or not hasattr(task, "uncancel"):
238	        return
239	    while task.cancelling():
240	        task.uncancel()

254	    try:
255	        _log_phase("launching", f"name_prefix={name_prefix} upload_mode={upload_mode} timeout={timeout}")
256	        pod = await guard.launch()
257	        _log_pod_identity(pod)
258	        _log_phase("waiting_ssh", f"pod_id={pod.id}")
259	        await pod.wait_ready(timeout=300)
260	        ssh_details = await pod._ensure_ssh_details()
261	        print(f"pod_ssh=root@{ssh_details['ip']} -p {ssh_details['port']}", flush=True)
262	        _log_phase("gpu_check", f"pod_id={pod.id}")
263	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh("nvidia-smi -L", timeout=60)
264	        print(stdout.strip(), flush=True)
265	        if code != 0:
266	            print(stderr, flush=True)
267	            result_code = code
268	            return result_code
269	
270	        if upload_mode == "tarball":
271	            await _upload_tarball(pod, exclude)
272	        else:
273	            _log_phase("uploading", "mode=sftp_walk")
274	            client = pod.open_ssh_client()
275	            try:
276	                sftp = client.open_sftp()
277	                try:
278	                    progress = UploadHeartbeat(label="sftp_upload")
279	                    upload_dir(sftp, ROOT, REMOTE_ROOT, exclude, progress=progress)
280	                    progress.tick(force=True)
281	                finally:
282	                    sftp.close()
283	            finally:
284	                client.close()
285	        print("upload_complete=true", flush=True)
286	
287	        _log_phase("starting_remote", f"timeout={timeout}")
288	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh(remote_script, timeout=timeout)
289	        print(stdout, flush=True)
290	        if stderr.strip():
291	            print(stderr, flush=True)
292	        result_code = code
293	        return result_code
294	    except asyncio.CancelledError:
295	        _clear_current_task_cancellation()
296	        result_code = 130
297	        _log_phase("cancelled", "exit_code=130")
298	        return result_code
299	    finally:
300	        pod_id = getattr(guard.pod, "id", "")
301	        _log_phase("terminating", f"pod_id={pod_id}" if pod_id else "")
302	        await guard.terminate()
303	        print("terminated_launched_pod=true", flush=True)
304	        if result_code is not None:
305	            _log_phase("done", f"exit_code={result_code}")
306	
307	
308	async def run_pod_detached(
309	    remote_script: str,
310	    *,
311	    name_prefix: str,
312	    exclude: set[str],
313	    upload_mode: Literal["sftp_walk", "tarball"] = "sftp_walk",
314	    timeout: int,
315	    poll_interval: int = 60,
316	) -> int:
317	    guard = PodGuard(name_prefix=name_prefix, default_max_runtime_seconds=max(timeout * 2, 7200))
318	    install_signal_handlers(asyncio.get_running_loop())
319	    result_code: int | None = None
320	    artifact_root: Path | None = None
321	    upload_info: dict[str, Any] = {"mode": upload_mode}
322	    remote_command: str | None = None
323	    terminated = False
324	    try:
325	        _log_phase("launching", f"name_prefix={name_prefix} upload_mode={upload_mode} timeout={timeout}")
326	        pod = await guard.launch()
327	        _log_pod_identity(pod)
328	        _log_phase("waiting_ssh", f"pod_id={pod.id}")
329	        await pod.wait_ready(timeout=300)
330	        ssh_details = await pod._ensure_ssh_details()
331	        print(f"pod_ssh=root@{ssh_details['ip']} -p {ssh_details['port']}", flush=True)
332	        _log_phase("gpu_check", f"pod_id={pod.id}")
333	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh("nvidia-smi -L", timeout=60)
334	        print(stdout.strip(), flush=True)
335	        if code != 0:
336	            print(stderr, flush=True)
337	            result_code = code
338	            return result_code
339	
340	        if upload_mode == "tarball":
341	            upload_info = await _upload_tarball(pod, exclude)
342	        else:
343	            _log_phase("uploading", "mode=sftp_walk")
344	            client = pod.open_ssh_client()
345	            try:
346	                sftp = client.open_sftp()
347	                try:
348	                    progress = UploadHeartbeat(label="sftp_upload")
349	                    upload_dir(sftp, ROOT, REMOTE_ROOT, exclude, progress=progress)
350	                    progress.tick(force=True)
351	                finally:
352	                    sftp.close()
353	            finally:
354	                client.close()
355	        print("upload_complete=true", flush=True)
356	
357	        _log_phase("starting_remote", "mode=detached")
358	        await _upload_remote_script(pod, remote_script)
359	        launch_command = (
360	            f"cd {REMOTE_ROOT} && mkdir -p out/corpus_matrix "
361	            "&& rm -f out/corpus_matrix/exit_code "
362	            "&& nohup bash /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-run.sh "
363	            "> /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-live.log 2>&1; "
364	            'rc=$?; printf "%s" "$rc" > out/corpus_matrix/exit_code; exit "$rc"'
365	        )
366	        remote_command = launch_command
367	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh(f"nohup bash -lc {launch_command!r} >/tmp/vibecomfy-launch.log 2>&1 & echo $!", timeout=30)
368	        if stdout.strip():
369	            print(f"remote_pid={stdout.strip()}", flush=True)
370	        if code != 0:
371	            print(stderr, flush=True)
372	            result_code = code
373	            return result_code
374	
375	        _log_phase("polling", f"interval_seconds={poll_interval} timeout={timeout}")
376	        start = time.monotonic()
377	        last_snapshot = ""
378	        while True:
379	            if time.monotonic() - start > timeout:
380	                print(f"detached_timeout={timeout}", flush=True)
381	                result_code = 124
382	                return result_code
383	            status_command = f"""
384	cd {REMOTE_ROOT}
385	echo "=== POLL $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ) ==="
386	if [ -f out/corpus_matrix/results.tsv ]; then echo "--- RESULTS ---"; cat out/corpus_matrix/results.tsv; fi
387	if [ -f out/corpus_matrix/ready_results.tsv ]; then echo "--- READY ---"; cat out/corpus_matrix/ready_results.tsv; fi
388	echo "--- MEDIA ---"
389	find out/corpus_matrix output -maxdepth 5 -type f \\( -name '*.png' -o -name '*.mp4' -o -name '*.webp' -o -name '*.webm' \\) -printf '%TY-%Tm-%Td %TH:%TM %s %p\\n' 2>/dev/null | sort | tail -80
390	echo "--- LOG ---"
391	tail -80 /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-live.log 2>/dev/null || true
392	tail -80 out/corpus_matrix/live.log 2>/dev/null || true
393	echo "--- EXIT ---"
394	cat out/corpus_matrix/exit_code 2>/dev/null || true
395	"""
396	            try:
397	                code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh(status_command, timeout=60)
398	            except Exception as exc:
399	                print(f"poll_ssh_failed={exc}", flush=True)
400	                await asyncio.sleep(poll_interval)
401	                continue
402	            snapshot = stdout.strip()
403	            if snapshot and snapshot != last_snapshot:
404	                print(snapshot, flush=True)
405	                last_snapshot = snapshot
406	            if stderr.strip():
407	                print(stderr, flush=True)
408	            if code != 0:
409	                result_code = code
410	                return result_code
411	            exit_code = _parse_detached_exit(stdout)
412	            if exit_code is not None:
413	                result_code = exit_code
414	                try:
415	                    artifact_root = await _download_artifacts(
416	                        pod,
417	                        exit_code=exit_code,
418	                        remote_command=remote_command,
419	                        upload=upload_info,
420	                    )
421	                except OSError as exc:
422	                    print(f"artifact_download_failed={exc}", flush=True)
423	                return result_code
424	            await asyncio.sleep(poll_interval)
425	    except asyncio.CancelledError:
426	        _clear_current_task_cancellation()
427	        result_code = 130
428	        _log_phase("cancelled", "exit_code=130")
429	        return result_code
430	    finally:
431	        pod_id = getattr(guard.pod, "id", "")
432	        _log_phase("terminating", f"pod_id={pod_id}" if pod_id else "")
433	        await guard.terminate()
434	        terminated = True
435	        print("terminated_launched_pod=true", flush=True)
436	        if result_code is not None:
437	            if artifact_root is not None:
438	                _finalize_artifacts(
439	                    artifact_root,
440	                    pod_id=pod_id or None,
441	                    exit_code=result_code,
442	                    mode="detached",
443	                    terminated=terminated,
444	                    remote_command=remote_command,
445	                    upload=upload_info,
446	                )
447	            _log_phase("done", f"exit_code={result_code}")
448	            _print_detached_summary(
449	                pod_id=pod_id or None,
450	                exit_code=result_code,
451	                terminated=terminated,
452	                artifact_root=artifact_root,
453	            )
454	
455	
456	def _runpod_lifecycle():
457	    import sys
458	
459	    sys.path.insert(0, str(runpod_lifecycle_root() / "src"))
460	    from dotenv import load_dotenv
461	    from runpod_lifecycle import RunPodConfig, launch
462	
463	    return load_dotenv, RunPodConfig, launch
464	
465	
466	async def _upload_remote_script(pod, remote_script: str) -> None:
467	    handle = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(prefix="vibecomfy-remote-run-", suffix=".sh", mode="w", delete=False)
468	    script_path = Path(handle.name)
469	    try:
470	        handle.write(remote_script)
471	        handle.write("\n")
472	        handle.close()
473	        client = pod.open_ssh_client()
474	        try:
475	            sftp = client.open_sftp()
476	            try:
477	                sftp.put(str(script_path), "/tmp/vibecomfy-remote-run.sh", confirm=False)
478	            finally:
479	                sftp.close()
480	        finally:
481	            client.close()
482	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh("chmod +x /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-run.sh", timeout=30)
483	        if code != 0:
484	            print(stdout, flush=True)
485	            print(stderr, flush=True)
486	            raise RuntimeError(f"remote script chmod failed with exit code {code}")
487	    finally:
488	        script_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
489	
490	
491	async def _download_artifacts(
492	    pod,
493	    *,
494	    exit_code: int | None = None,
495	    remote_command: str | None = None,
496	    upload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
497	) -> Path | None:
498	    local_root = _new_artifact_root()
499	    local_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
500	    remote_archive = "/tmp/vibecomfy-artifacts.tar.gz"
501	    _log_phase("downloading_artifacts", f"local={local_root.relative_to(ROOT)}")
502	    code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh(
503	        f"cd {REMOTE_ROOT} || exit $?; "
504	        "mkdir -p out/corpus_matrix; "
505	        "cp /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-live.log out/corpus_matrix/remote_live.log 2>/dev/null || true; "
506	        "cp /tmp/vibecomfy-remote-run.sh out/corpus_matrix/remote_run.sh 2>/dev/null || true; "
507	        "paths=''; "
508	        "for path in out/corpus_matrix output out/runs; do if [ -e \"$path\" ]; then paths=\"$paths $path\"; fi; done; "
509	        "if [ -z \"$paths\" ]; then mkdir -p /tmp/vibecomfy-empty-artifacts && "
510	        f"tar -czf {remote_archive} -C /tmp/vibecomfy-empty-artifacts .; "
511	        f"elif ! tar -czf {remote_archive} $paths 2>/tmp/vibecomfy-artifact-tar.err; then "
512	        "cat /tmp/vibecomfy-artifact-tar.err; exit 1; fi",
513	        timeout=300,
514	    )
515	    if code != 0:
516	        print(stdout, flush=True)
517	        if stderr.strip():
518	            print(stderr, flush=True)
519	        print("artifact_download_failed=archive", flush=True)
520	        return None
521	    client = pod.open_ssh_client()
522	    try:
523	        sftp = client.open_sftp()
524	        try:
525	            archive = local_root / "artifacts.tar.gz"
526	            sftp.get(remote_archive, str(archive))
527	        finally:
528	            sftp.close()
529	    except Exception as exc:
530	        print(f"artifact_download_failed={exc}", flush=True)
531	        return None
532	    finally:
533	        client.close()
534	    with tarfile.open(local_root / "artifacts.tar.gz", "r:gz") as tar:
535	        tar.extractall(local_root)
536	    _finalize_artifacts(
537	        local_root,
538	        pod_id=getattr(pod, "id", None),
539	        exit_code=exit_code,
540	        mode="detached",
541	        remote_command=remote_command,
542	        upload=upload,
543	    )
544	    print(f"artifact_downloaded={local_root.relative_to(ROOT)}", flush=True)
545	    return local_root
546	
547	
548	def _new_artifact_root() -> Path:
549	    stamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
550	    root = ROOT / "out" / "runpod_artifacts" / stamp
551	    if not root.exists():
552	        return root
553	    suffix = 1
554	    while True:
555	        candidate = ROOT / "out" / "runpod_artifacts" / f"{stamp}-{suffix}"
556	        if not candidate.exists():
557	            return candidate
558	        suffix += 1
559	
560	
561	def _parse_tsv(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
562	    try:
563	        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
564	            reader = csv.DictReader(handle, delimiter="\t")
565	            if not reader.fieldnames:
566	                return []
567	            rows: list[dict[str, str]] = []
568	            for row in reader:
569	                rows.append({str(key): "" if value is None else value for key, value in row.items() if key is not None})
570	            return rows
571	    except OSError:
572	        return []
573	
574	
575	def _image_info(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
576	    try:
577	        from PIL import Image
578	    except Exception:
579	        return _png_info(path)
580	    try:
581	        with Image.open(path) as image:
582	            return {
583	                "width": image.width,
584	                "height": image.height,
585	                "format": image.format,
586	                "mode": image.mode,
587	            }
588	    except Exception:
589	        return None
590	
591	
592	def _png_info(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
593	    if path.suffix.lower() != ".png":
594	        return None
595	    try:
596	        with path.open("rb") as handle:
597	            header = handle.read(24)
598	    except OSError:
599	        return None
600	    if len(header) < 24 or header[:8] != b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" or header[12:16] != b"IHDR":
601	        return None
602	    width, height = struct.unpack(">II", header[16:24])
603	    return {
604	        "width": width,
605	        "height": height,
606	        "format": "PNG",
607	        "mode": None,
608	    }
609	
610	
611	def _finalize_artifacts(
612	    local_root: Path,
613	    *,
614	    pod_id: str | None = None,
615	    exit_code: int | None = None,
616	    mode: str = "detached",
617	    terminated: bool | None = None,
618	    remote_command: str | None = None,
619	    upload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
620	) -> dict[str, Any]:
621	    manifest = _build_artifact_manifest(
622	        local_root,
623	        pod_id=pod_id,
624	        exit_code=exit_code,
625	        mode=mode,
626	        terminated=terminated,
627	        remote_command=remote_command,
628	        upload=upload,
629	    )
630	    manifest_path = local_root / "manifest.json"
631	    manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True, default=str) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
632	    report_path = _write_artifact_report(local_root, manifest)
633	    manifest["manifest_path"] = _display_path(manifest_path)
634	    manifest["report_path"] = _display_path(report_path)
635	    manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True, default=str) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
636	    return manifest
637	
638	
639	def _build_artifact_manifest(
640	    local_root: Path,
641	    *,
642	    pod_id: str | None = None,
643	    exit_code: int | None = None,
644	    mode: str = "detached",
645	    terminated: bool | None = None,
646	    remote_command: str | None = None,
647	    upload: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
648	) -> dict[str, Any]:
649	    loaded_manifest = _load_json(local_root / "manifest.json")
650	    existing = loaded_manifest if isinstance(loaded_manifest, dict) else {}
651	    generated_at = existing.get("generated_at") or datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
652	    archive = local_root / "artifacts.tar.gz"
653	    results_path = local_root / "out" / "corpus_matrix" / "results.tsv"
654	    results = _parse_tsv(results_path)
655	    outputs = _collect_outputs(local_root)
656	    run_metadata, metadata_warnings = _collect_run_metadata(local_root)
657	    watchdogs, watchdog_warnings = _collect_watchdogs(local_root, run_metadata)
658	    remote_logs = _collect_remote_logs(local_root)
659	    remote_script = _file_record(local_root / "out" / "corpus_matrix" / "remote_run.sh", local_root)
660	    failures = _count_failures(results)
661	    warnings = metadata_warnings + watchdog_warnings
662	    status = "unknown"
663	    if exit_code is not None:
664	        status = "pass" if exit_code == 0 and failures == 0 else "fail"
665	    manifest: dict[str, Any] = {
666	        "generated_at": generated_at,
667	        "pod_id": pod_id,
668	        "mode": mode,
669	        "remote_root": REMOTE_ROOT,
670	        "artifact_root": _display_path(local_root),
671	        "exit_code": exit_code,
672	        "terminated": terminated,
673	        "status": status,
674	        "summary": {
675	            "status": status,
676	            "outputs": len(outputs),
677	            "result_rows": len(results),
678	            "failures": failures,
679	            "warnings": len(warnings),
680	            "exit_code": exit_code,
681	            "terminated": terminated,
682	        },
683	        "archive": _file_record(archive, local_root) if archive.exists() else None,
684	        "upload": upload or {},
685	        "remote_command": remote_command,
686	        "remote_script": remote_script,
687	        "results": {
688	            "path": _display_path(results_path) if results_path.exists() else None,
689	            "rows": results,
690	        },
691	        "outputs": outputs,
692	        "run_metadata": run_metadata,
693	        "watchdogs": watchdogs,
694	        "remote_logs": remote_logs,
695	        "warnings": warnings,
696	    }
697	    return manifest
698	
699	
700	def _collect_outputs(local_root: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
701	    output_root = local_root / "output"
702	    if not output_root.exists():
703	        return []
704	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
705	    for path in sorted(item for item in output_root.rglob("*") if item.is_file()):
706	        record = _file_record(path, local_root)
707	        if record is None:
708	            continue
709	        record["output_relative_path"] = path.relative_to(output_root).as_posix()
710	        info = _image_info(path)
711	        if info is not None:
712	            record["image"] = info
713	        records.append(record)
714	    return records
715	
716	
717	def _collect_run_metadata(local_root: Path) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str]]:
718	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
719	    warnings: list[str] = []
720	    runs_root = local_root / "out" / "runs"
721	    if not runs_root.exists():
722	        return records, warnings
723	    for path in sorted(runs_root.glob("*/metadata.json")):
724	        data = _load_json(path)
725	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
726	            warnings.append(f"invalid run metadata: {_display_path(path)}")
727	            continue
728	        queued = data.get("queued")
729	        prompt_id = _extract_prompt_id(queued)
730	        outputs = data.get("outputs") if isinstance(data.get("outputs"), list) else []
731	        records.append(
732	            {
733	                "path": _display_path(path),
734	                "relative_path": path.relative_to(local_root).as_posix(),
735	                "run_id": data.get("run_id") or path.parent.name,
736	                "workflow_id": data.get("workflow_id"),
737	                "runtime": data.get("runtime"),
738	                "prompt_id": prompt_id,
739	                "outputs": outputs,
740	                "workflow_hash": data.get("workflow_hash"),
741	                "git_sha": data.get("git_sha"),
742	                "metadata": data,
743	            }
744	        )
745	    return records, warnings
746	
747	
748	def _collect_watchdogs(
749	    local_root: Path,
750	    run_metadata: list[dict[str, Any]],
751	) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], list[str]]:
752	    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
753	    warnings: list[str] = []
754	    outputs_by_run = {
755	        str(record.get("run_id")): bool(record.get("outputs"))
756	        for record in run_metadata
757	        if record.get("run_id")
758	    }
759	    runs_root = local_root / "out" / "runs"
760	    if not runs_root.exists():
761	        return records, warnings
762	    for path in sorted(runs_root.glob("*/watchdog.json")):
763	        data = _load_watchdog_json(path)
764	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
765	            warnings.append(f"invalid watchdog report: {_display_path(path)}")
766	            continue
767	        state = data.get("state") if isinstance(data.get("state"), dict) else {}
768	        run_id = path.parent.name
769	        diagnosis = data.get("diagnosis")
770	        stop_reason = state.get("stop_reason")
771	        record = {
772	            "path": _display_path(path),
773	            "relative_path": path.relative_to(local_root).as_posix(),
774	            "run_id": run_id,
775	            "diagnosis": diagnosis,
776	            "diagnosis_reason": data.get("diagnosis_reason"),
777	            "stop_reason": stop_reason,
778	            "elapsed_seconds": data.get("elapsed_seconds"),
779	            "prompt_id": state.get("prompt_id"),
780	            "current_node_id": state.get("current_node_id"),
781	            "current_node_class_type": state.get("current_node_class_type"),
782	            "state": state,
783	        }
784	        records.append(record)
785	        if diagnosis == "crashed" and stop_reason == "completed" and outputs_by_run.get(run_id):
786	            warnings.append(
787	                f"watchdog diagnosis=crashed for run_id={run_id} but stop_reason=completed and outputs exist"
788	            )
789	    return records, warnings
790	
791	
792	def _collect_remote_logs(local_root: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
793	    candidates = [
794	        local_root / "out" / "corpus_matrix" / "remote_live.log",
795	        local_root / "out" / "corpus_matrix" / "live.log",
796	        local_root / "out" / "corpus_matrix" / "remote_run.sh",
797	    ]
798	    runs_root = local_root / "out" / "runs"
799	    if runs_root.exists():
800	        candidates.extend(sorted(runs_root.glob("*/*.log")))
801	    seen: set[Path] = set()
802	    logs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
803	    for path in candidates:
804	        if path in seen or not path.exists() or not path.is_file():
805	            continue
806	        seen.add(path)
807	        record = _file_record(path, local_root)
808	        if record is not None:
809	            logs.append(record)
810	    return logs

923	def _print_detached_summary(
924	    *,
925	    pod_id: str | None,
926	    exit_code: int,
927	    terminated: bool,
928	    artifact_root: Path | None,
929	) -> None:
930	    manifest: dict[str, Any] = {}
931	    if artifact_root is not None:
932	        loaded = _load_json(artifact_root / "manifest.json")
933	        if isinstance(loaded, dict):
934	            manifest = loaded
935	    summary = manifest.get("summary") if isinstance(manifest.get("summary"), dict) else {}
936	    failures = int(summary.get("failures") or 0)
937	    outputs = int(summary.get("outputs") or 0)
938	    status = summary.get("status") or ("pass" if exit_code == 0 and failures == 0 else "fail")
939	    print(f"status={status} exit_code={exit_code}", flush=True)
940	    if pod_id:
941	        print(f"pod_id={pod_id}", flush=True)
942	    print(f"terminated={str(terminated).lower()}", flush=True)
943	    if artifact_root is not None:
944	        print(f"artifact_dir={_display_path(artifact_root)}", flush=True)
945	        print(f"manifest={_display_path(artifact_root / 'manifest.json')}", flush=True)
946	        print(f"report={_display_path(artifact_root / 'report.md')}", flush=True)
947	    print(f"outputs={outputs}", flush=True)
948	    print(f"failures={failures}", flush=True)

950	
951	def _file_record(path: Path, root: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
952	    try:
953	        stat = path.stat()
954	    except OSError:
955	        return None
956	    return {
957	        "path": _display_path(path),
958	        "relative_path": path.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
959	        "bytes": stat.st_size,
960	        "extension": path.suffix.lower(),
961	        "sha256": _sha256(path),
962	    }
963	
964	
965	def _sha256(path: Path) -> str | None:
966	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
967	    try:
968	        with path.open("rb") as handle:
969	            for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
970	                digest.update(chunk)
971	    except OSError:
972	        return None
973	    return digest.hexdigest()
974	
975	
976	def _load_json(path: Path) -> Any:
977	    try:
978	        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
979	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
980	        return None
981	
982	
983	def _load_watchdog_json(path: Path) -> Any:
984	    try:
985	        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
986	    except OSError:
987	        return None
988	    try:
989	        return json.loads(text)
990	    except json.JSONDecodeError:
991	        start = text.find("{")
992	        if start < 0:
993	            return None
994	        try:
995	            return json.loads(text[start:])
996	        except json.JSONDecodeError:
997	            return None
998	
999	
1000	def _extract_prompt_id(value: Any) -> str | None:
1001	    if isinstance(value, dict):
1002	        direct = value.get("prompt_id")
1003	        if isinstance(direct, str):
1004	            return direct
1005	        for item in value.values():
1006	            found = _extract_prompt_id(item)
1007	            if found:
1008	                return found
1009	    if isinstance(value, list):
1010	        for item in value:
1011	            found = _extract_prompt_id(item)
1012	            if found:
1013	                return found
1014	    if isinstance(value, str):
1015	        match = re.search(r"(?:prompt_id['\"]?\s*[:=]\s*['\"]?)([A-Za-z0-9_.:-]+)", value)
1016	        if match:
1017	            return match.group(1)
1018	    return None
1019	
1020	
1021	def _count_failures(rows: list[dict[str, str]]) -> int:
1022	    passing = {"ok", "pass", "passed", "success", "succeeded"}
1023	    failures = 0
1024	    for row in rows:
1025	        status = (row.get("status") or row.get("result") or "").strip().lower()
1026	        if status and status not in passing:
1027	            failures += 1
1028	    return failures
1029	
1030	
1031	def _display_path(path: Path) -> str:
1032	    try:
1033	        return path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix()
1034	    except ValueError:
1035	        return path.as_posix()
1036	
1037	
1038	def _md(value: Any) -> str:
1039	    return str(value).replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ")
1040	
1041	
1042	def _parse_detached_exit(stdout: str) -> int | None:
1043	    lines = stdout.splitlines()
1044	    for index, line in enumerate(lines):
1045	        if line == "--- EXIT ---" and index + 1 < len(lines):
1046	            value = lines[index + 1].strip()
1047	            if value.isdigit():
1048	                return int(value)
1049	    return None

1051	
1052	async def _upload_tarball(pod, exclude: set[str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
1053	    tar_path = _build_upload_tarball(exclude)
1054	    upload_info = {
1055	        "mode": "tarball",
1056	        "local_archive_path": tar_path.as_posix(),
1057	        "remote_archive_path": "/tmp/vibecomfy-upload.tar.gz",
1058	        "archive_bytes": tar_path.stat().st_size,
1059	        "excludes": sorted(exclude),
1060	    }
1061	    try:
1062	        _log_phase("uploading", f"mode=tarball size={_format_bytes(tar_path.stat().st_size)}")
1063	        client = pod.open_ssh_client()
1064	        try:
1065	            sftp = client.open_sftp()
1066	            try:
1067	                progress = UploadHeartbeat(label="tarball_upload")
1068	
1069	                def _progress(sent: int, total: int) -> None:
1070	                    progress.bytes = sent
1071	                    progress.files = 1
1072	                    now = time.monotonic()
1073	                    if sent == total or now - progress.last_log >= progress.every_seconds:
1074	                        print(
1075	                            f"tarball_upload_progress bytes={sent} total_bytes={total} "
1076	                            f"size={_format_bytes(sent)} total_size={_format_bytes(total)}",
1077	                            flush=True,
1078	                        )
1079	                        progress.last_log = now
1080	
1081	                sftp.put(str(tar_path), "/tmp/vibecomfy-upload.tar.gz", callback=_progress, confirm=False)
1082	            finally:
1083	                sftp.close()
1084	        finally:
1085	            client.close()
1086	        _log_phase("extracting", f"remote_root={REMOTE_ROOT}")
1087	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh(
1088	            f"rm -rf {REMOTE_ROOT} && mkdir -p {REMOTE_ROOT} && tar --no-same-owner -xzf /tmp/vibecomfy-upload.tar.gz -C {REMOTE_ROOT}",
1089	            timeout=300,
1090	        )
1091	        if code != 0:
1092	            print(stdout, flush=True)
1093	            print(stderr, flush=True)
1094	            raise RuntimeError(f"remote tarball extraction failed with exit code {code}")
1095	        return upload_info
1096	    finally:
1097	        tar_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
1098	
1099	
1100	def _upload_tmpdir() -> Path:
1101	    configured = os.getenv("VIBECOMFY_UPLOAD_TMPDIR")
1102	    temp_dir = Path(configured).expanduser() if configured else Path(tempfile.gettempdir())
1103	    temp_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
1104	    return temp_dir
1105	
1106	
1107	def _iter_upload_files(root: Path, exclude: set[str]) -> Iterable[Path]:
1108	    for current, dir_names, file_names in os.walk(root):
1109	        current_path = Path(current)
1110	        dir_names[:] = [
1111	            name for name in dir_names if not should_skip(current_path / name, root, exclude)
1112	        ]
1113	        for file_name in file_names:
1114	            path = current_path / file_name
1115	            if should_skip(path, root, exclude):
1116	                continue
1117	            yield path
1118	
1119	
1120	def _estimate_upload_payload(root: Path, exclude: set[str]) -> tuple[list[Path], int]:
1121	    files: list[Path] = []
1122	    total_bytes = 0
1123	    heartbeat = UploadHeartbeat(label="upload_scan")
1124	    for path in _iter_upload_files(root, exclude):
1125	        try:
1126	            size = path.stat().st_size
1127	        except OSError:
1128	            continue
1129	        files.append(path)
1130	        total_bytes += size
1131	        heartbeat.tick(files=1, bytes_added=size)
1132	    heartbeat.tick(force=True)
1133	    return files, total_bytes
1134	
1135	
1136	def _preflight_upload_disk(temp_dir: Path, estimated_bytes: int) -> None:
1137	    usage = shutil.disk_usage(temp_dir)
1138	    min_free_bytes = int(os.getenv("VIBECOMFY_UPLOAD_MIN_FREE_BYTES", str(512 * MiB)))
1139	    required_free = max(min_free_bytes, int(estimated_bytes * 0.25))
1140	    if usage.free >= required_free:
1141	        return
1142	    raise RuntimeError(
1143	        "insufficient local disk for RunPod upload tarball: "
1144	        f"tmpdir={temp_dir} free={_format_bytes(usage.free)} "
1145	        f"estimated_payload={_format_bytes(estimated_bytes)} required_free={_format_bytes(required_free)}. "
1146	        "Free disk space, set VIBECOMFY_UPLOAD_TMPDIR to a larger volume, or add excludes for bulky local paths."
1147	    )
1148	
1149	
1150	def _build_upload_tarball(exclude: set[str], *, root: Path = ROOT) -> Path:
1151	    _log_phase("building_upload", f"mode=tarball root={root}")
1152	    temp_dir = _upload_tmpdir()
1153	    files, estimated_bytes = _estimate_upload_payload(root, exclude)
1154	    _log_phase(
1155	        "building_upload",
1156	        f"files={len(files)} estimated_payload={_format_bytes(estimated_bytes)} tmpdir={temp_dir}",
1157	    )
1158	    _preflight_upload_disk(temp_dir, estimated_bytes)
1159	
1160	    handle = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(prefix="vibecomfy-upload-", suffix=".tar.gz", dir=temp_dir, delete=False)
1161	    tar_path = Path(handle.name)
1162	    handle.close()
1163	    heartbeat = UploadHeartbeat(label="tarball_build")
1164	    try:
1165	        with tarfile.open(tar_path, "w:gz") as tar:
1166	            for path in files:
1167	                tar.add(path, arcname=path.relative_to(root))
1168	                try:
1169	                    size = path.stat().st_size
1170	                except OSError:
1171	                    size = 0
1172	                heartbeat.tick(files=1, bytes_added=size)
1173	        heartbeat.tick(force=True)
1174	        _log_phase("building_upload", f"archive={tar_path} size={_format_bytes(tar_path.stat().st_size)}")
1175	    except Exception:
1176	        tar_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
1177	        raise
1178	    return tar_path
1179

1	"""Async facade for interacting with a single RunPod pod lifecycle."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import asyncio
6	import logging
7	import os
8	import time
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	from . import api
12	from .config import RunPodConfig
13	from .errors import LaunchFailure, NotReadyTimeout, SSHError, TerminateError
14	from .events import EventHooks, PodState, _emit_error, _emit_state
15	from .ssh import SSHClient
16	from .storage import STORAGE_CHECK_COMMAND, evaluate_storage_health, parse_df_output
17	
18	logger = logging.getLogger("runpod_lifecycle.pod")
19	
20	
21	class Pod:
22	    """Async operations for a specific RunPod pod."""
23	
24	    def __init__(
25	        self,
26	        pod_id: str,
27	        name: str,
28	        config: RunPodConfig,
29	        hooks: EventHooks | None = None,
30	        ram_tier: int = 0,
31	        storage_volume: str | None = None,
32	    ) -> None:
33	        self.id = pod_id
34	        self.name = name
35	        self.config = config
36	        self.hooks = hooks or EventHooks()
37	        self._ram_tier = ram_tier
38	        self._storage_volume = storage_volume
39	        self._ssh_details: dict[str, Any] | None = None
40	        self._last_exec_at: float | None = None
41	        self._created_at = time.monotonic()
42	
43	    async def wait_ready(self, timeout: int = 600) -> dict[str, Any]:
44	        start_time = time.monotonic()
45	        poll_interval = 5
46	        current_state: PodState | None = None
47	
48	        while True:
49	            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start_time
50	            if elapsed > timeout:
51	                raise NotReadyTimeout(f"Pod {self.id} did not become ready within {timeout} seconds")
52	
53	            status = await self.status()
54	            desired_status = status.get("desired_status") if status else None
55	            ports = (status.get("ports") or []) if status else []
56	
57	            if current_state is None:
58	                current_state = PodState.PROVISIONING
59	                await _emit_state(self.hooks, self.id, PodState.PROVISIONING, {"status": status or {}})
60	
61	            if desired_status in {"FAILED", "TERMINATED"}:
62	                failed_state = PodState.TERMINATED if desired_status == "TERMINATED" else PodState.FAILED
63	                await _emit_state(self.hooks, self.id, failed_state, {"status": status or {}})
64	                raise LaunchFailure(f"Pod {self.id} entered terminal state {desired_status}")
65	
66	            has_ssh_port = any(port.get("privatePort") == 22 for port in ports)
67	            if desired_status == "RUNNING" and has_ssh_port:
68	                if current_state is not PodState.READY:
69	                    self._ssh_details = await self._ensure_ssh_details()
70	                    current_state = PodState.READY
71	                    await _emit_state(self.hooks, self.id, PodState.READY, {"status": status})
72	                return status
73	
74	            if current_state is not PodState.STARTING:
75	                current_state = PodState.STARTING
76	                await _emit_state(self.hooks, self.id, PodState.STARTING, {"status": status or {}})
77	
78	            await asyncio.sleep(poll_interval)
79	
80	    async def status(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
81	        return await asyncio.to_thread(api.get_pod_status, self.id, self.config.api_key)
82	
83	    async def exec_ssh(self, cmd: str, timeout: int = 600) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
84	        ssh_details = await self._ensure_ssh_details()
85	        ssh_client = self._build_ssh_client(ssh_details)
86	
87	        def _run_command() -> tuple[int, str, str]:
88	            ssh_client.connect()
89	            try:
90	                return ssh_client.execute_command(cmd, timeout)
91	            finally:
92	                ssh_client.disconnect()
93	
94	        try:
95	            result = await asyncio.to_thread(_run_command)
96	        except Exception as exc:
97	            raise SSHError(f"SSH command failed for pod {self.id}: {exc}") from exc
98	
99	        self._last_exec_at = time.monotonic()
100	        return result
101	
102	    async def is_idle(self, threshold_seconds: int) -> bool:
103	        last_activity = self._last_exec_at or self._created_at
104	        if time.monotonic() - last_activity < threshold_seconds:
105	            return False
106	
107	        try:
108	            exit_code, stdout, _stderr = await self.exec_ssh(
109	                "nvidia-smi --query-gpu=utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader,nounits"
110	            )
111	            if exit_code != 0:
112	                return False
113	
114	            first_line = stdout.strip().splitlines()[0]
115	            return int(first_line) < 5
116	        except (IndexError, ValueError, SSHError) as exc:
117	            logger.warning("Could not determine GPU utilization for pod %s: %s", self.id, exc)
118	            return False
119	
120	    async def terminate(self) -> None:
121	        try:
122	            await asyncio.to_thread(api.terminate_pod, self.id, self.config.api_key)
123	        except Exception as exc:
124	            await _emit_error(self.hooks, exc, {"pod_id": self.id})
125	            raise TerminateError(f"Failed to terminate pod {self.id}: {exc}") from exc
126	
127	        await _emit_state(self.hooks, self.id, PodState.TERMINATED, {"pod_id": self.id})
128	
129	    async def check_storage_health(
130	        self,
131	        min_free_gb: int = 50,
132	        max_percent_used: int = 85,
133	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
134	        _exit_code, raw_output, _stderr = await self.exec_ssh(STORAGE_CHECK_COMMAND, timeout=30)
135	        parsed = parse_df_output(raw_output)
136	
137	        api_total_gb = None
138	        if self._storage_volume:
139	            volumes = await asyncio.to_thread(api.get_network_volumes, self.config.api_key)
140	            volume_info = next((volume for volume in volumes if volume.get("id") == self._storage_volume), None)
141	            api_total_gb = volume_info.get("size") if volume_info else None
142	
143	        return evaluate_storage_health(parsed, api_total_gb, min_free_gb, max_percent_used)
144	
145	    def open_ssh_client(self) -> Any:
146	        """Open and return the underlying connected paramiko SSH client."""
147	        ssh_details = self._ensure_ssh_details_sync()
148	        ssh_client = self._build_ssh_client(ssh_details)
149	        ssh_client.connect()
150	        raw_client = getattr(ssh_client, "client", None)
151	        if raw_client is None:
152	            raise SSHError(f"SSH client for pod {self.id} did not expose a raw client")
153	        return raw_client
154	
155	    async def _ensure_ssh_details(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
156	        return await asyncio.to_thread(self._ensure_ssh_details_sync)
157	
158	    def _ensure_ssh_details_sync(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
159	        if self._ssh_details is None:
160	            self._ssh_details = api.get_pod_ssh_details(self.id, self.config.api_key)
161	
162	        if not self._ssh_details:
163	            raise SSHError(f"Could not get SSH details for pod {self.id}")
164	        return self._ssh_details
165	
166	    def _build_ssh_client(self, ssh_details: dict[str, Any]) -> SSHClient:
167	        if self.config.ssh_private_key:
168	            return SSHClient(
169	                hostname=ssh_details["ip"],
170	                port=ssh_details["port"],
171	                username="root",
172	                private_key_content=self.config.ssh_private_key,
173	            )
174	
175	        if self.config.ssh_private_key_path:
176	            expanded_path = os.path.expanduser(self.config.ssh_private_key_path)
177	            if os.path.exists(expanded_path):
178	                return SSHClient(
179	                    hostname=ssh_details["ip"],
180	                    port=ssh_details["port"],
181	                    username="root",
182	                    private_key_path=self.config.ssh_private_key_path,
183	                )
184	
185	        return SSHClient(
186	            hostname=ssh_details["ip"],
187	            port=ssh_details["port"],
188	            username="root",
189	            [REDACTED]("password", "runpod"),
190	        )
191

1	"""Configuration primitives for the standalone RunPod lifecycle package."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import json
6	import os
7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
8	from typing import Any
9	
10	from dotenv import load_dotenv
11	
12	DEFAULT_GPU_TYPE = "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090"
13	DEFAULT_WORKER_IMAGE = "runpod/pytorch:2.4.0-py3.11-cuda12.4.1-devel-ubuntu22.04"
14	DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID = "runpod-torch-v240"
15	DEFAULT_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH = "/workspace"
16	DEFAULT_RAM_TIERS = (72, 60, 48, 32, 16)
17	
18	
19	def _parse_bool(value: str | None, default: bool) -> bool:
20	    if value is None:
21	        return default
22	    return value.strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
23	
24	
25	def _parse_int(value: str | None, default: int) -> int:
26	    if value is None or value.strip() == "":
27	        return default
28	    return int(value)
29	
30	
31	def _parse_csv_tuple(value: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
32	    if value is None or value.strip() == "":
33	        return ()
34	    return tuple(part.strip() for part in value.split(",") if part.strip())
35	
36	
37	def _parse_int_tuple(value: str | None, default: tuple[int, ...]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
38	    parts = _parse_csv_tuple(value)
39	    if not parts:
40	        return default
41	    return tuple(int(part) for part in parts)
42	
43	
44	def _parse_env_vars(value: str | None) -> dict[str, str]:
45	    if value is None or value.strip() == "":
46	        return {}
47	    parsed = json.loads(value)
48	    if not isinstance(parsed, dict):
49	        raise ValueError("RUNPOD_ENV_VARS must decode to a JSON object")
50	    return {str(key): str(item) for key, item in parsed.items()}
51	
52	
53	@dataclass(slots=True)
54	class RunPodConfig:
55	    api_key: str
56	    gpu_type: str = DEFAULT_GPU_TYPE
57	    worker_image: str = DEFAULT_WORKER_IMAGE
58	    template_id: str = DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID
59	    volume_mount_path: str = DEFAULT_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH
60	    disk_size_gb: int = 200
61	    container_disk_gb: int = 200
62	    min_vcpu_count: int = 8
63	    min_memory_gb: int = 32
64	    ram_tiers_enabled: bool = True
65	    ram_tiers: tuple[int, ...] = DEFAULT_RAM_TIERS
66	    storage_volumes: tuple[str, ...] = ()
67	    storage_name: str | None = None
68	    ssh_public_key: str | None = None
69	    ssh_private_key: str | None = None
70	    ssh_public_key_path: str | None = None
71	    ssh_private_key_path: str | None = None
72	    env_vars: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
73	    name_prefix: str = "pod"
74	
75	    @classmethod
76	    def from_env(cls, **overrides: Any) -> "RunPodConfig":
77	        load_dotenv()
78	
79	        data: dict[str, Any] = {
80	            "api_key": os.getenv("RUNPOD_API_KEY"),
81	            "gpu_type": os.getenv("RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE", DEFAULT_GPU_TYPE),
82	            "worker_image": os.getenv("RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE", DEFAULT_WORKER_IMAGE),
83	            "template_id": os.getenv("RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID", DEFAULT_TEMPLATE_ID),
84	            "volume_mount_path": os.getenv("RUNPOD_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH", DEFAULT_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH),
85	            "disk_size_gb": _parse_int(os.getenv("RUNPOD_DISK_SIZE_GB"), 200),
86	            "container_disk_gb": _parse_int(os.getenv("RUNPOD_CONTAINER_DISK_GB"), 200),
87	            "min_vcpu_count": _parse_int(os.getenv("RUNPOD_MIN_VCPU_COUNT"), 8),
88	            "min_memory_gb": _parse_int(os.getenv("RUNPOD_MIN_MEMORY_GB"), 32),
89	            "ram_tiers_enabled": _parse_bool(
90	                os.getenv("RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS_ENABLED", os.getenv("RUNPOD_RAM_TIER_FALLBACK")),
91	                True,
92	            ),
93	            "ram_tiers": _parse_int_tuple(os.getenv("RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS"), DEFAULT_RAM_TIERS),
94	            "storage_volumes": _parse_csv_tuple(os.getenv("RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES")),
95	            "storage_name": os.getenv("RUNPOD_STORAGE_NAME"),
96	            "ssh_public_key": os.getenv("RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY"),
97	            "ssh_private_key": os.getenv("RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY"),
98	            "ssh_public_key_path": os.getenv("RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY_PATH"),
99	            "ssh_private_key_path": os.getenv("RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH"),
100	            "env_vars": _parse_env_vars(os.getenv("RUNPOD_ENV_VARS")),
101	            "name_prefix": os.getenv("RUNPOD_NAME_PREFIX", "pod"),
102	        }
103	        data.update(overrides)
104	
105	        if not data.get("api_key"):
106	            raise ValueError("RUNPOD_API_KEY environment variable is required")
107	
108	        return cls(**data)
109	
110	    def merge(self, **overrides: Any) -> "RunPodConfig":
111	        return replace(self, **overrides)

2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4

7	import json

17	
18	def _resolve_api_key(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str:
19	    key = getattr(args, "api_key", None) or os.getenv("RUNPOD_API_KEY")
20	    if not key:
21	        print("error: RUNPOD_API_KEY not set (use --api-key or .env)", file=sys.stderr)
22	        sys.exit(2)
23	    return key
24	
25	
26	def _parse_duration(text: str) -> int:
27	    m = re.fullmatch(r"\s*(\d+)\s*([smhd]?)\s*", text)
28	    if not m:
29	        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError(f"invalid duration: {text!r}")
30	    n, unit = int(m.group(1)), m.group(2) or "s"
31	    return n * {"s": 1, "m": 60, "h": 3600, "d": 86400}[unit]
32	
33	
34	def _summary_to_row(s: discovery.PodSummary) -> list[str]:
35	    return [
36	        s.id,
37	        s.name or "-",
38	        s.desired_status or "-",
39	        s.gpu_type or "-",
40	        f"{s.uptime_seconds}s" if s.uptime_seconds is not None else "-",
41	        f"${s.cost_per_hr:.3f}/hr",
42	    ]
43	
44	
45	def _print_table(rows: list[list[str]], headers: list[str]) -> None:
46	    if not rows:
47	        print("(no pods)")
48	        return
49	    widths = [max(len(h), *(len(r[i]) for r in rows)) for i, h in enumerate(headers)]
50	    fmt = "  ".join(f"{{:<{w}}}" for w in widths)
51	    print(fmt.format(*headers))
52	    print(fmt.format(*["-" * w for w in widths]))
53	    for r in rows:
54	        print(fmt.format(*r))
55	
56	
57	def _print_cost(summaries: list[discovery.PodSummary]) -> None:
58	    cost = discovery.cost_summary(summaries)
59	    print(
60	        f"\nTotal: ${cost['total_per_hr']:.3f}/hr  "
61	        f"(daily ${cost['daily']:.2f}, monthly ${cost['monthly']:.2f})"
62	    )
63	
64	
65	async def _cmd_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
66	    [REDACTED](args)
67	    pods = await discovery.list_pods(api_key, name_prefix=args.name_prefix)
68	    if args.json:
69	        print(json.dumps([p.__dict__ for p in pods], default=str, indent=2))
70	        return 0
71	    _print_table(
72	        [_summary_to_row(p) for p in pods],
73	        ["ID", "NAME", "STATUS", "GPU", "UPTIME", "COST"],
74	    )
75	    _print_cost(pods)
76	    return 0
77	
78	
79	async def _cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:

82	    if not status:

84	        return 1

89	async def _cmd_terminate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:

92	        confirm = input(f"terminate pod {args.pod_id}? [y/N] ").strip().lower()
93	        if confirm != "y":
94	            print("aborted")

97	    print(f"terminated {args.pod_id}")

100	
101	async def _cmd_find_orphans(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:

107	    orphans = await discovery.find_orphans(

116	    )
117	    _print_cost(orphans)
118	    if args.terminate and orphans:
119	        if not args.yes:
120	            confirm = input(f"\nterminate {len(orphans)} orphan(s)? [y/N] ").strip().lower()
121	            if confirm != "y":
122	                print("aborted")
123	                return 1
124	        for p in orphans:
125	            try:
126	                await discovery.terminate(p.id, api_key)
127	                print(f"terminated {p.id}")
128	            except Exception as exc:
129	                print(f"failed to terminate {p.id}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
130	    return 0

133	async def _cmd_gpu_types(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
134	    [REDACTED](args)
135	    sdk = api._get_runpod()
136	    sdk.api_key = api_key
137	    gpus = await asyncio.to_thread(sdk.get_gpus)
138	    if args.json:

140	    else:

143	    return 0
144	
145	
146	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
147	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="runpod-lifecycle", description="RunPod pod lifecycle CLI.")
148	    parser.add_argument("--api-key", help="Override RUNPOD_API_KEY env var.")
149	    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
150	
151	    p_list = sub.add_parser("list", help="List all pods on the account.")
152	    p_list.add_argument("--name-prefix", help="Filter to pods whose name starts with PREFIX.")
153	    p_list.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
154	
155	    p_status = sub.add_parser("status", help="Show normalized status for a pod.")
156	    p_status.add_argument("pod_id")
157	
158	    p_term = sub.add_parser("terminate", help="Terminate a pod.")
159	    p_term.add_argument("pod_id")
160	    p_term.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip confirmation.")
161	
162	    p_orph = sub.add_parser("find-orphans", help="Find pods on the account not in the supplied known-ids list.")
163	    p_orph.add_argument("--known-ids-file", help="File with one pod id per line. Empty if omitted.")
164	    p_orph.add_argument("--older-than", type=_parse_duration, default=None,
165	                        help="Only orphans with uptime >= this duration (e.g. 1h, 30m, 90s).")
166	    p_orph.add_argument("--name-prefix", help="Filter pods to those whose name starts with PREFIX.")
167	    p_orph.add_argument("--terminate", action="store_true", help="After listing, terminate each orphan.")
168	    p_orph.add_argument("--yes", "-y", action="store_true", help="Skip terminate confirmation.")
169	
170	    p_gpu = sub.add_parser("gpu-types", help="List available GPU types from RunPod.")
171	    p_gpu.add_argument("--json", action="store_true")
172	
173	    return parser
174	
175	
176	_HANDLERS = {
177	    "list": _cmd_list,
178	    "status": _cmd_status,
179	    "terminate": _cmd_terminate,
180	    "find-orphans": _cmd_find_orphans,
181	    "gpu-types": _cmd_gpu_types,
182	}
183	
184	
185	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
186	    load_dotenv()
187	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
188	    handler = _HANDLERS[args.cmd]
189	    try:
190	        return asyncio.run(handler(args))
191	    except KeyboardInterrupt:
192	        return 130
193	    except Exception as exc:
194	        print(f"error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
195	        return 1
196	
197	
198	if __name__ == "__main__":
199	    raise SystemExit(main())

1	"""Launch orchestration for RunPod pods with RAM-tier and storage fallback."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import asyncio
6	import logging
7	import os
8	import time
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	from .api import create_pod, find_gpu_type, get_network_volumes
12	from .config import RunPodConfig
13	from .errors import LaunchFailure
14	from .events import EventHooks, PodState, _emit_error, _emit_state
15	from .pod import Pod
16	from .storage import check_and_expand_storage, get_storage_volume_id
17	
18	logger = logging.getLogger("runpod_lifecycle.lifecycle")
19	
20	
21	def _resolve_public_key_string(config: RunPodConfig) -> str | None:
22	    if config.ssh_public_key:
23	        return config.ssh_public_key
24	
25	    if config.ssh_public_key_path:
26	        expanded_path = os.path.expanduser(config.ssh_public_key_path)
27	        try:
28	            return open(expanded_path, "r", encoding="utf-8").read().strip()
29	        except OSError as exc:
30	            logger.warning("Could not read SSH public key from %s: %s", expanded_path, exc)
31	
32	    logger.warning("No SSH public key configured; pod access may require password auth")
33	    return None
34	
35	
36	def _build_ram_tiers(config: RunPodConfig) -> list[int]:
37	    if not config.ram_tiers_enabled:
38	        return [config.min_memory_gb]
39	
40	    ram_tiers = [tier for tier in config.ram_tiers if tier >= config.min_memory_gb]
41	    return ram_tiers or [config.min_memory_gb]
42	
43	
44	async def _resolve_storage_targets(config: RunPodConfig) -> list[tuple[str | None, str | None]]:
45	    raw_targets: list[str] = []
46	    for storage_name in [config.storage_name, *config.storage_volumes]:
47	        if storage_name and storage_name not in raw_targets:
48	            raw_targets.append(storage_name)
49	
50	    if not raw_targets:
51	        return [(None, None)]
52	
53	    resolved_targets: list[tuple[str | None, str | None]] = []
54	    for storage_name in raw_targets:
55	        volume_id = await asyncio.to_thread(get_storage_volume_id, config.api_key, storage_name)
56	        if volume_id:
57	            resolved_targets.append((storage_name, volume_id))
58	        else:
59	            logger.warning("Storage '%s' not found, skipping", storage_name)
60	
61	    return resolved_targets
62	
63	
64	async def launch(
65	    config: RunPodConfig,
66	    *,
67	    name: str | None = None,
68	    hooks: EventHooks | None = None,
69	) -> Pod:
70	    hooks = hooks or EventHooks()
71	    pod_name = name or f"{config.name_prefix}-{int(time.time())}"
72	
73	    await _emit_state(hooks, None, PodState.PROVISIONING, {"name": pod_name})
74	
75	    gpu_info = await asyncio.to_thread(find_gpu_type, config.gpu_type, config.api_key)
76	    if not gpu_info:
77	        error = LaunchFailure(f"RunPod GPU type '{config.gpu_type}' could not be resolved")
78	        await _emit_error(hooks, error, {"gpu_type": config.gpu_type, "name": pod_name})
79	        raise error
80	
81	    public_key_string = _resolve_public_key_string(config)
82	    ram_tiers = _build_ram_tiers(config)
83	    storage_targets = await _resolve_storage_targets(config)
84	
85	    input_storages = [value for value in [config.storage_name, *config.storage_volumes] if value]
86	    if input_storages and not storage_targets:
87	        error = LaunchFailure(
88	            f"Configured storage volumes could not be resolved: {', '.join(input_storages)}"
89	        )
90	        await _emit_error(
91	            hooks,
92	            error,
93	            {"storages": input_storages, "name": pod_name},
94	        )
95	        raise error
96	
97	    expanded_storage_ids: set[str] = set()
98	    attempted_pairs: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
99	    last_error: Exception | None = None
100	
101	    for ram_tier in ram_tiers:
102	        for storage_name, storage_volume_id in storage_targets:
103	            attempted_pairs.append(
104	                {
105	                    "ram_tier": ram_tier,
106	                    "storage_name": storage_name,
107	                    "storage_volume_id": storage_volume_id,
108	                }
109	            )
110	
111	            if storage_volume_id and storage_volume_id not in expanded_storage_ids:
112	                await asyncio.to_thread(
113	                    check_and_expand_storage,
114	                    config.api_key,
115	                    storage_volume_id,
116	                    50,
117	                    storage_name,
118	                )
119	                expanded_storage_ids.add(storage_volume_id)
120	
121	            try:
122	                pod_details = await asyncio.to_thread(
123	                    create_pod,
124	                    api_key=config.api_key,
125	                    gpu_type_id=gpu_info["id"],
126	                    image_name=config.worker_image,
127	                    name=pod_name,
128	                    network_volume_id=storage_volume_id,
129	                    volume_mount_path=config.volume_mount_path,
130	                    disk_in_gb=config.disk_size_gb,
131	                    container_disk_in_gb=config.container_disk_gb,
132	                    public_key_string=public_key_string,
133	                    env_vars=config.env_vars,
134	                    min_vcpu_count=config.min_vcpu_count,
135	                    min_memory_in_gb=ram_tier,
136	                    template_id=config.template_id,
137	                )
138	            except Exception as exc:
139	                last_error = exc
140	                error_message = str(exc).lower()
141	                if "no longer any instances available" in error_message:
142	                    logger.warning(
143	                        "No instances available for storage=%s ram=%sGB",
144	                        storage_name,
145	                        ram_tier,
146	                    )
147	                else:
148	                    logger.warning(
149	                        "Pod creation failed for storage=%s ram=%sGB: %s",
150	                        storage_name,
151	                        ram_tier,
152	                        exc,
153	                    )
154	                continue
155	
156	            pod = Pod(
157	                pod_id=pod_details["id"],
158	                name=pod_name,
159	                config=config,
160	                hooks=hooks,
161	                ram_tier=ram_tier,
162	                storage_volume=storage_volume_id,
163	            )
164	            await _emit_state(
165	                hooks,
166	                pod.id,
167	                PodState.PROVISIONING,
168	                {
169	                    "name": pod_name,
170	                    "ram_tier": ram_tier,
171	                    "storage_name": storage_name,
172	                    "storage_volume_id": storage_volume_id,
173	                    "pod_details": pod_details,
174	                },
175	            )
176	            return pod
177	
178	    if last_error is None:
179	        last_error = LaunchFailure("No pod launch attempts were made")
180	
181	    error = LaunchFailure(
182	        f"Failed to create pod after trying RAM tiers {ram_tiers} across storages "
183	        f"{[pair['storage_name'] for pair in attempted_pairs] or [None]}: {last_error}"
184	    )
185	    await _emit_error(
186	        hooks,
187	        error,
188	        {
189	            "name": pod_name,
190	            "ram_tiers": ram_tiers,
191	            "attempted_pairs": attempted_pairs,
192	            "last_error": str(last_error),
193	        },
194	    )
195	    raise error from last_error
196	
197	
198	__all__ = ["find_gpu_type", "get_network_volumes", "launch"]
199
```

1	"""Event hook primitives for consumers that persist pod state externally."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import inspect
6	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
7	from enum import Enum
8	from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable
9	
10	
11	class PodState(str, Enum):
12	    PROVISIONING = "PROVISIONING"
13	    STARTING = "STARTING"
14	    READY = "READY"
15	    STOPPED = "STOPPED"
16	    FAILED = "FAILED"
17	    TERMINATED = "TERMINATED"
18	
19	
20	@dataclass(slots=True)
21	class PodEvent:
22	    pod_id: str | None
23	    state: PodState
24	    detail: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
25	
26	
27	StateHook = Callable[[PodEvent], Awaitable[None] | None]
28	ErrorHook = Callable[[Exception, dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[None] | None]
29	
30	
31	@dataclass(slots=True)
32	class EventHooks:
33	    on_state_change: StateHook | None = None
34	    on_error: ErrorHook | None = None
35	
36	
37	async def _maybe_await(result: object) -> None:
38	    if inspect.iscoroutine(result):
39	        await result
40	
41	
42	async def _emit_state(
43	    hooks: EventHooks | None,
44	    pod_id: str | None,
45	    state: PodState,
46	    detail: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
47	) -> None:
48	    if hooks is None or hooks.on_state_change is None:
49	        return
50	    result = hooks.on_state_change(PodEvent(pod_id=pod_id, state=state, detail=detail or {}))
51	    await _maybe_await(result)
52	
53	
54	async def _emit_error(
55	    hooks: EventHooks | None,
56	    error: Exception,
57	    detail: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
58	) -> None:
59	    if hooks is None or hooks.on_error is None:
60	        return
61	    result = hooks.on_error(error, detail or {})
62	    await _maybe_await(result)
63
```

1	"""Account-wide RunPod discovery, attach-by-id, orphan detection, and module-level termination."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import asyncio
6	import logging
7	from dataclasses import dataclass
8	from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable
9	
10	from . import api
11	from .config import RunPodConfig
12	from .errors import LaunchFailure, TerminateError
13	from .events import EventHooks, PodState, _emit_error, _emit_state
14	from .pod import Pod
15	
16	logger = logging.getLogger("runpod_lifecycle.discovery")
17	
18	
19	@dataclass(frozen=True)
20	class PodSummary:
21	    id: str
22	    name: str | None
23	    desired_status: str | None
24	    actual_status: str | None
25	    gpu_type: str | None
26	    image: str | None
27	    created_at: str | None
28	    cost_per_hr: float
29	    uptime_seconds: int | None
30	    ports: list[dict[str, Any]]
31	    network_volume_id: str | None
32	
33	
34	def _to_summary(raw: dict[str, Any]) -> PodSummary:
35	    runtime = raw.get("runtime") if isinstance(raw.get("runtime"), dict) else {}
36	    machine = raw.get("machine") if isinstance(raw.get("machine"), dict) else {}
37	    cost = raw.get("costPerHr")
38	    try:
39	        cost_val = float(cost) if cost is not None else 0.0
40	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
41	        cost_val = 0.0
42	    return PodSummary(
43	        id=str(raw.get("id") or ""),
44	        name=raw.get("name"),
45	        desired_status=raw.get("desiredStatus"),
46	        actual_status=raw.get("actualStatus"),
47	        gpu_type=raw.get("machineType") or machine.get("gpuDisplayName") or machine.get("gpuTypeId"),
48	        image=raw.get("imageName"),
49	        created_at=raw.get("createdAt"),
50	        cost_per_hr=cost_val,
51	        uptime_seconds=runtime.get("uptimeInSeconds") if runtime else None,
52	        ports=runtime.get("ports", []) if runtime else [],
53	        network_volume_id=raw.get("networkVolumeId"),
54	    )
55	
56	
57	def _list_pods_sync(api_key: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
58	    sdk = api._get_runpod()
59	    sdk.api_key = api_key
60	    pods = sdk.get_pods()
61	    return list(pods) if pods else []
62	
63	
64	async def list_pods(api_key: str, *, name_prefix: str | None = None) -> list[PodSummary]:
65	    """Return summaries for every pod on the RunPod account, optionally filtered by name prefix."""
66	    raw = await asyncio.to_thread(_list_pods_sync, api_key)
67	    summaries = [_to_summary(p) for p in raw if isinstance(p, dict)]
68	    if name_prefix:
69	        summaries = [s for s in summaries if s.name and s.name.startswith(name_prefix)]
70	    return summaries
71	
72	
73	async def find_pods(
74	    api_key: str,
75	    predicate: Callable[[PodSummary], bool],
76	    *,
77	    name_prefix: str | None = None,
78	) -> list[PodSummary]:
79	    """Return account pods matching the caller-supplied predicate."""
80	    summaries = await list_pods(api_key, name_prefix=name_prefix)
81	    return [s for s in summaries if predicate(s)]
82	
83	
84	_ACTIVE_STATUSES = {"RUNNING", "PROVISIONING"}
85	
86	
87	async def find_orphans(
88	    api_key: str,
89	    known_pod_ids: Iterable[str],
90	    *,
91	    name_prefix: str | None = None,
92	    older_than_seconds: int | None = None,
93	) -> list[PodSummary]:
94	    """Return active pods on the RunPod account that the caller doesn't know about.
95	
96	    Mirrors the orphan partition logic from
97	    reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/debug/commands/runpod.py:46-70.
98	    `known_pod_ids` is supplied by the caller (e.g. orchestrator's DB query);
99	    this package never touches Supabase.
100	    """
101	    known = {str(pid) for pid in known_pod_ids if pid}
102	    summaries = await list_pods(api_key, name_prefix=name_prefix)
103	    orphans = [s for s in summaries if s.desired_status in _ACTIVE_STATUSES and s.id not in known]
104	    if older_than_seconds is not None:
105	        orphans = [
106	            s for s in orphans if s.uptime_seconds is not None and s.uptime_seconds >= older_than_seconds
107	        ]
108	    return orphans
109	
110	
111	async def get_pod(
112	    pod_id: str,
113	    config: RunPodConfig,
114	    *,
115	    hooks: EventHooks | None = None,
116	    name: str | None = None,
117	) -> Pod:
118	    """Attach to an existing pod by id. Raises LaunchFailure if it doesn't exist."""
119	    status = await asyncio.to_thread(api.get_pod_status, pod_id, config.api_key)
120	    if not status:
121	        raise LaunchFailure(f"Pod {pod_id} not found on RunPod account")
122	    pod = Pod(
123	        pod_id=pod_id,
124	        name=name or pod_id,
125	        config=config,
126	        hooks=hooks,
127	        ram_tier=0,
128	        storage_volume=None,
129	    )
130	    return pod
131	
132	
133	async def terminate(
134	    pod_id: str,
135	    api_key: str,
136	    *,
137	    hooks: EventHooks | None = None,
138	) -> None:
139	    """Terminate a pod by id without needing a Pod handle. Raises TerminateError on failure."""
140	    try:
141	        await asyncio.to_thread(api.terminate_pod, pod_id, api_key)
142	    except Exception as exc:
143	        await _emit_error(hooks, exc, {"pod_id": pod_id, "operation": "terminate"})
144	        raise TerminateError(f"Failed to terminate pod {pod_id}: {exc}") from exc
145	    await _emit_state(hooks, pod_id, PodState.TERMINATED, {"source": "discovery.terminate"})
146	
147	
148	def cost_summary(pods: Iterable[PodSummary]) -> dict[str, float]:
149	    """Aggregate $/hr across pods. Mirrors _print_orphaned_pods cost math."""
150	    total = sum(p.cost_per_hr for p in pods)
151	    return {
152	        "total_per_hr": round(total, 4),
153	        "daily": round(total * 24, 2),
154	        "monthly": round(total * 24 * 30, 2),
155	    }
156
