```
1	# runpod-lifecycle
2	
3	`runpod_lifecycle` is a small async package for the full RunPod pod lifecycle: launch a pod, wait until SSH is ready, run commands, inspect status and idleness, monitor storage health, and terminate cleanly without pulling in orchestrator-specific state management.
4	
5	## Install
6	
7	```bash
8	pip install -e .[dev]
9	```
10	
11	From another project, install directly from GitHub instead of relying on a
12	sibling checkout:
13	
14	```bash
15	pip install "runpod-lifecycle @ git+https://github.com/banodoco/runpod-lifecycle.git@v0.1.1"
16	```
17	
18	## Environment Variables
19	
20	`RunPodConfig.from_env()` reads these variables:
21	
22	- `RUNPOD_API_KEY`
23	- `RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE`
24	- `RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE`
25	- `RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID`
26	- `RUNPOD_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH`
27	- `RUNPOD_DISK_SIZE_GB`
28	- `RUNPOD_CONTAINER_DISK_GB`
29	- `RUNPOD_MIN_VCPU_COUNT`
30	- `RUNPOD_MIN_MEMORY_GB`
31	- `RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS_ENABLED`
32	- `RUNPOD_RAM_TIER_FALLBACK` (legacy alias for `RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS_ENABLED`)
33	- `RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS`
34	- `RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES`
35	- `RUNPOD_STORAGE_NAME`
36	- `RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY`
37	- `RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY`
38	- `RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY_PATH`
39	- `RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH`
40	- `RUNPOD_ENV_VARS`
41	- `RUNPOD_NAME_PREFIX`
42	
43	## Quick Start
44	
45	```python
46	import asyncio
47	
48	from runpod_lifecycle import RunPodConfig, launch, EventHooks
49	
50	
51	async def on_state(event):
52	    print(f"{event.state}: pod_id={event.pod_id} detail={event.detail}")
53	
54	
55	async def main() -> None:
56	    cfg = RunPodConfig.from_env(
57	        storage_name="my-network-volume",
58	    )
59	    hooks = EventHooks(on_state_change=on_state)
60	
61	    pod = await launch(cfg, hooks=hooks)
62	    await pod.wait_ready(timeout=600)
63	
64	    exit_code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh("nvidia-smi -L", timeout=60)
65	    print(exit_code)
66	    print(stdout)
67	    print(stderr)
68	
69	    await pod.terminate()
70	
71	
72	asyncio.run(main())
73	```
74	
75	If `storage_name` is unset and `storage_volumes` is empty, `launch()` creates a volumeless pod by passing `network_volume_id=None`. That is intentional new behavior in this package.
76	
77	For direct file transport or other low-level SSH work, `Pod.open_ssh_client()` returns a connected `paramiko`-compatible client. Callers are responsible for closing the returned client when they are done with it.
78	
79	## Config Reference
80	
81	| field | env var | default | description |
82	| --- | --- | --- | --- |
83	| `api_key` | `RUNPOD_API_KEY` | required | RunPod API key used by all SDK and HTTP calls. |
84	| `gpu_type` | `RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE` | `NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090` | Display name used by `find_gpu_type()` before pod creation. |
85	| `worker_image` | `RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE` | `runpod/pytorch:2.4.0-py3.11-cuda12.4.1-devel-ubuntu22.04` | Container image passed to RunPod at launch time. |
86	| `template_id` | `RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID` | `runpod-torch-v240` | RunPod template identifier used when creating the pod. |
87	| `volume_mount_path` | `RUNPOD_VOLUME_MOUNT_PATH` | `/workspace` | Mount path for an attached network volume inside the container. |
88	| `disk_size_gb` | `RUNPOD_DISK_SIZE_GB` | `20` | Root disk size requested for the pod. |
89	| `container_disk_gb` | `RUNPOD_CONTAINER_DISK_GB` | `50` | Container disk size requested for the pod. |
90	| `min_vcpu_count` | `RUNPOD_MIN_VCPU_COUNT` | `8` | Minimum vCPU count passed to RunPod when launching. |
91	| `min_memory_gb` | `RUNPOD_MIN_MEMORY_GB` | `32` | Lowest RAM target allowed for launch fallback. |
92	| `ram_tiers_enabled` | `RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS_ENABLED` | `True` | Enables RAM-tier fallback instead of launching only at `min_memory_gb`. |
93	| `ram_tiers` | `RUNPOD_RAM_TIERS` | `(72, 60, 48, 32, 16)` | Ordered RAM fallback tiers; values below `min_memory_gb` are filtered out. |
94	| `storage_volumes` | `RUNPOD_STORAGE_VOLUMES` | `()` | Ordered fallback list of storage names to resolve and try after `storage_name`. |
95	| `storage_name` | `RUNPOD_STORAGE_NAME` | `None` | Preferred storage name to try first before `storage_volumes`. |
96	| `ssh_public_key` | `RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY` | `None` | Inline SSH public key string injected into the pod environment. |
97	| `ssh_private_key` | `RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY` | `None` | Inline private key used by `Pod.exec_ssh()`. |
98	| `ssh_public_key_path` | `RUNPOD_SSH_PUBLIC_KEY_PATH` | `None` | Filesystem path to the public key if not provided inline. |
99	| `ssh_private_key_path` | `RUNPOD_SSH_PRIVATE_KEY_PATH` | `None` | Filesystem path to the private key if not provided inline. |
100	| `env_vars` | `RUNPOD_ENV_VARS` | `{}` | JSON object of extra environment variables sent when the pod is created. |
101	| `name_prefix` | `RUNPOD_NAME_PREFIX` | `pod` | Prefix used for generated pod names when `launch(..., name=...)` is not provided. |
102	
103	## Scope Notes
104	
105	This package does not include `startup_script.py`, `check_worker_startup_status`, or any orchestrator-owned persistence layer. Consumers that need to persist lifecycle state should attach `EventHooks(on_state_change=..., on_error=...)` and write to their own database or control plane there.
106
```

```
1	# Seinfeld Scene Generator — Project Plan
2	
3	A demo of LTX 2.3 that generates Seinfeld-style scenes from a concept. Built on top of a fine-tuned LoRA, agent-written scripts, and Astrid's timeline composition.
4	
5	## The spine: a shared vocabulary
6	
7	One thing connects every stage: a **locked vocabulary** of scenes, characters, costumes, and shot types. Captions use it. Training learns it. Script-to-shot decomposition emits it. Inference prompts consume it. Get this right once, in a single element, and every downstream stage stops being a separate consistency problem.
8	
9	Concretely: ~3 scenes (Jerry's apt, Monk's diner, one more), ~4 characters with 1–2 outfit variants each, ~4 shot types. Small, formulaic, Seinfeld-shaped.
10	
11	## Four orchestrators in Astrid
12	
13	### 1. `dataset_build` — produce the training set
14	Owns the bucket-fill loop. Reads a **criteria element** (bucket targets + per-bucket inclusion rules + caption template + output schemas), runs until full.
15	
16	Steps, in order, wrapped in `repeat.until(buckets_full)`:
17	- `code`: YouTube search → candidate URLs
18	- `code`: yt-dlp video download (extend existing audio util)
19	- `code`: scene-segment (reuse `packs/builtin/scenes`)
20	- `code`: cheap pre-filter (length, transcript keywords)
21	- `attested` (agent): VLM judge → bucket assignment, schema-checked
22	- `attested` (agent): VLM caption using locked vocabulary, schema-checked
23	- **`attested` (human): review ~50 random clip+caption pairs** before training kicks off
24	- `code`: manifest export
25	
26	### 2. `lora_train` — train the LoRA
27	Takes the manifest, runs LTX 2.3 training using **[ai-toolkit by ostris](https://github.com/ostris/ai-toolkit)** as the training framework.
28	
29	Steps:
30	- `code`: provision a RunPod GPU instance (see "Cross-cutting infra" below — this should use a shared `runpod` executor at the Astrid level, not the RunPod plumbing currently buried inside vibecomfy)
31	- `code`: stage manifest + ai-toolkit config to the pod
32	- `code`: kick off training, stream logs back, pull checkpoints
33	- `code`: generate eval sample grid — same prompts run against baseline LTX and each candidate checkpoint, side-by-side
34	- **`attested` (human): review the eval grid and pick the winning checkpoint.** This is the "evals" gate — a human looks at samples and selects the LoRA that best hits the vocabulary/style targets. The produced artifact is a `chosen_checkpoint.json` with the path + notes; the gate verifies it before downstream orchestrators can use it.
35	- `code`: tear down the pod, register the promoted LoRA
36	
37	### 3. `script_to_shots` — concept → shot list
38	Two `attested` (agent) steps:
39	- Kimi K2.6: concept → script (dialogue + scene headers)
40	- Kimi K2.6: script → shot JSON, **constrained to the locked vocabulary** (only emits scenes/costumes that exist)
41	
42	The vocabulary constraint is what keeps script generation aligned with what the LoRA can render. Schema-check the shot JSON.
43	
44	### 4. `scene_render` — shots → final video
45	
46	The per-shot generation isn't single-shot — it's an **iteration loop with automated quality judging**, using the existing `video_understand` / `visual_understand` executors as judges.
47	
48	For each shot, wrapped in `repeat.until(passes_judge OR max_attempts)`:
49	- `code`: LTX+LoRA generates the clip via vibecomfy (`external.vibecomfy.run`), seed-varied per attempt
50	- `attested` (agent): `video_understand` judges the clip against the shot spec — does it show the right scene, character, costume, action? Does it look like Seinfeld? Returns a structured pass/fail + reasons, schema-checked.
51	- If fail and attempts remain: re-roll with a refined prompt (judge's reasons inform the refinement) or a new seed
52	- If max attempts hit: keep the best-scoring attempt and flag it for the human review gate
53	
54	After all shots are rendered:
55	- `code`: optional TTS or on-screen text for dialogue
56	- `code`: assemble clips into timeline composition in order
57	- **`attested` (human): review final cut, flag any shots to re-render before final render** — the human is mainly looking at the flagged shots from the per-shot loop, plus overall scene coherence
58	- `code`: render final video
59	
60	This is the same iteration pattern that ai-tooling generally needs: generate → automated judge → human gate on what the judge couldn't catch. `visual_understand` can be used the same way on extracted keyframes if you want a faster/cheaper first-pass judge before invoking the full `video_understand`.
61	
62	## How they compose
63	
64	A top-level `seinfeld_demo` orchestrator chains the four as `nested` steps:
65	
66	```
67	dataset_build → lora_train → script_to_shots → scene_render
68	```
69	
70	These aren't strictly serial in practice. `script_to_shots` and `scene_render` can be developed and iterated against the *baseline* LTX (no LoRA) before `lora_train` finishes — they only need the vocabulary, not the LoRA itself. That's the parallelization you want.
71	
72	## Astrid primitives recap
73	
74	- **Executor** = one unit of work (one network call, one transformation, one artifact in / one artifact out). The capability.
75	- **Orchestrator** = composes executors into a workflow. Owns loop logic, conditional branching, retries-across-stages.
76	- **Element** = reusable schema or render building block. The criteria spec and caption template live here.
77	
78	At task-run time, every step in a plan is one of:
79	
80	| Step kind | Who does it | How it advances |
81	|---|---|---|
82	| `code` | nobody — deterministic argv subprocess | runs, gate verifies `produces`, moves on |
83	| `attested` | an agent or a human | actor runs the work, acks with identity + evidence, gate verifies `produces` |
84	| `nested` | another orchestrator | delegates to a child plan |
85	
86	Human steps are first-class. An `attested` step with `--actor <name>` is a human step: the human does the work, runs the ack, and the gate validates the produced artifact against a schema before the run advances. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.
87	
88	Loops/fan-out are `repeat.until` / `repeat.for_each` wrappers on any step.
89	
90	## Cross-cutting infra: port RunPod up to Astrid
91	
92	RunPod provisioning currently lives inside vibecomfy. For this project (and beyond), it should be lifted up to an Astrid-level shared executor — something like `external.runpod.{provision,run,teardown}` — so any orchestrator can request a GPU pod, run work on it, and tear it down with the same plumbing.
93	
94	Why now:
95	- `lora_train` needs RunPod for ai-toolkit training runs
96	- `scene_render` needs RunPod for LTX+LoRA inference (when not local)
97	- Future GPU-bound executors (eval grids, larger captioning runs) will need the same
98	- Keeping it inside vibecomfy couples GPU lifecycle to one framework; making it a peer executor lets vibecomfy *consume* it like everything else
99	
100	This is a small refactor that pays back across every other workstream. Worth doing early in Phase 1.
101	
102	## What lives where
103	
104	- **Astrid**: criteria/vocabulary element, all four orchestrators, RunPod lifecycle executors (newly lifted from vibecomfy), all executors except the LTX/Kimi inference calls themselves
105	- **vibecomfy**: LTX inference workflow (with and without LoRA), invoked by Astrid executors; consumes the Astrid-level RunPod executor instead of owning that lifecycle
106	- **ai-toolkit (ostris)**: LTX 2.3 LoRA training, invoked by `lora_train` on a RunPod pod
107	- **Existing tools to reuse**: `packs/builtin/scenes`, `packs/builtin/video_understand`, `packs/builtin/visual_understand` (both as automated judges in iteration loops), possibly `pool_build` as a model for `dataset_build`, the existing YouTube audio util (extend to video), existing VLM capability
108	
109	## Sequencing
110	
111	**Phase 0 (1 day):** Lock the vocabulary. Write 10 example inference prompts you want to work. Reverse-engineer the caption template from those. Cheapest, highest-leverage hour in the project.
112	
113	**Phase 1 (parallel, ~1 week):**
114	- Lift RunPod lifecycle out of vibecomfy into a shared Astrid executor (foundational — unblocks training and inference)
115	- Build `dataset_build` orchestrator and missing executors
116	- Get baseline LTX 2.3 inference running through vibecomfy (now consuming the Astrid RunPod executor)
117	- Prototype `script_to_shots` against baseline LTX
118	
119	**Phase 2 (~1 week):**
120	- Run `dataset_build` end-to-end, human-review the dataset
121	- Wire up `lora_train` against ai-toolkit by ostris on RunPod
122	- Train LoRA, human picks the winning checkpoint from the eval grid
123	- Refine `script_to_shots` based on what baseline taught you
124	
125	**Phase 3 (~3–5 days):**
126	- Build `scene_render` with the per-shot iteration loop (`video_understand` as judge)
127	- Integrate timeline composition
128	- End-to-end demo: concept → finished scene
129	
130	## Risks, ranked
131	
132	1. **Vocabulary drift between caption and inference prompt.** Mitigation: same element generates both, no free-text prompts at inference.
133	2. **VLM cost on dataset construction.** Mitigation: aggressive pre-filter before VLM ever sees a clip (length, transcript keywords, scene-detect metadata).
134	3. **Character identity across cuts within one scene.** This is the one the LoRA may not fully solve. Mitigation: prompt-locking + small vocabulary. If still bad, accept as a known demo limitation or add per-character reference-image conditioning later.
135	4. **Script writes shots the LoRA can't render.** Mitigation: shot-JSON schema is enum-constrained to the vocabulary — Kimi physically can't emit out-of-vocabulary shots.
136	
137	## Unifying idea
138	
139	One small locked vocabulary, four orchestrators that all speak it, human gates at the two places that matter (dataset quality, final cut), everything else automated and verifiable through Astrid's `produces` checks.
140
```

1	"""Runtime entrypoint for external.vibecomfy.*."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import argparse
6	import subprocess
7	import sys
8	from pathlib import Path
9	
10	
11	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
12	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run VibeComfy workflow commands.")
13	    parser.add_argument("command", choices=("run", "validate"))
14	    parser.add_argument("workflow", type=Path)
15	    return parser
16	
17	
18	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
19	    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
20	    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "vibecomfy.cli", args.command, str(args.workflow)]).returncode
21	
22	
23	if __name__ == "__main__":
24	    raise SystemExit(main())
25
```

6	# VibeComfy

8	Curated Astrid executor metadata for invoking VibeComfy workflow actions through

16	the folder-level `PACKAGE_ID`.
17

1	# Creating Tools
2	
3	Use this guide when Astrid is missing a capability.
4	
5	## Operating Level
6	
7	Start with the highest-level command that fits the user request. For normal
8	video creation, run an orchestrator through `python3 -m astrid` instead of
9	chaining internal executors by hand.
10	
11	Do not chain pipeline internals by hand unless you are debugging one specific
12	stage. Source-analysis executors intentionally pass file artifacts such as
13	transcripts, scenes, quote candidates, pools, timelines, and assets. Those files
14	make runs resumable and auditable, but they are not the right interface for a
15	creative request like "make a video about AI". Use the hype orchestrator or add
16	a new orchestrator for that workflow.
17	
18	Current start points:
19	
20	```bash
21	# Source-backed edit
22	python3 -m astrid --video source.mp4 --brief brief.txt --out runs/example --render
23	
24	# Audio-backed edit
25	python3 -m astrid --audio voiceover.wav --brief brief.txt --out runs/audio --render
26	
27	# Pure-generative edit from an existing brief
28	python3 -m astrid --brief examples/briefs/cinematic.txt --out runs/generative --render --target-duration 15
29	```
30	
31	If the user gives a topic instead of a brief, create or use a brief-generation
32	executor, then coordinate it from an orchestrator. Do not fake source media just
33	to satisfy a source-video path.
34	
35	## Build Order
36	
37	Before adding anything, follow this order. Move to the next step only when the
38	previous one cannot satisfy the request.
39	
40	1. **Try to compose existing executors.** Run `python3 -m astrid executors
41	   list` and `inspect` the likely candidates. If a workflow can be built by
42	   wiring existing executors together, write *only* an orchestrator that calls
43	   them. Do not duplicate logic that already lives in an executor.
44	2. **Create the missing executors.** Each new executor must do exactly one
45	   concrete unit of work — independently runnable, inspectable, testable. Keep
46	   it narrow: one network call, one transformation, one artifact in / one
47	   artifact out. Workflow shape, retries-across-stages, and conditional
48	   branching belong in the orchestrator, not the executor.
49	3. **Write the orchestrator that composes them.** It calls the executors
50	   (existing + new) and may call other orchestrators. Executors must not call
51	   orchestrators.
52	
53	Anti-pattern: a single orchestrator `run.py` that opens HTTP sockets, parses
54	model output, downloads files, and assembles grids — all inline. That is three
55	or four executors hiding in a trench coat. Split them out so each piece is
56	discoverable, reusable, and individually testable.
57	
58	## Decision Rule
59	
60	Create an **executor** when the missing capability performs one concrete unit of
61	work. It should be independently runnable, inspectable, and testable. Examples:
62	fetch Reigh data, render a timeline, upload a video, inspect audio, build a
63	sprite sheet, generate a brief from a topic, or transform one artifact into
64	another.
65	
66	Create an **orchestrator** when the missing capability coordinates a workflow.
67	It should call or plan child executors/orchestrators and keep business flow out
68	of individual tool implementations. Examples: hype pipeline, event-talk
69	workflow, thumbnail workflow, topic-to-video creation, or an understanding
70	dispatcher.
71	
72	Create an **element** when the missing capability is a reusable render building
73	block consumed by timeline JSON. Effects, animations, and transitions are
74	elements. If the user needs an editable visual primitive, fork or create an
75	element instead of hard-coding behavior in an executor.
76	
77	Create a **shared library** only when the code has no public runtime of its own.
78	Shared hype/editing concepts belong under `astrid/domains/hype`. Generic
79	plumbing belongs under `astrid/utilities`. Executor-specific helpers belong
80	inside that executor's optional `src/` package.
81	
82	For a one-off experiment, keep outputs and scratch files under `runs/`. Do not
83	create a public executor, orchestrator, or element unless the behavior should be
84	discoverable and reusable.
85	
86	## Common Friction Points
87	
88	**Too many required file paths.** This is expected for low-level executors.
89	Those paths are the artifact contract. Solve it by using an orchestrator, adding
90	a small helper executor for the missing artifact, or adding an orchestrator that
91	owns the whole flow. Only add literal/stdin conveniences when direct executor
92	use is itself the product surface.
93	
94	**Pool building rejects abstract or dialogue-light sources.** The source-video
95	hype path expects usable visual and dialogue candidates. If the goal is
96	abstract or purely generative, use the pure-generative path. If source-backed
97	abstract editing should be reusable, add an explicit orchestrator mode or a
98	focused executor change with tests rather than hand-editing triage and quote
99	JSON to force a pool.
100	
101	**No brief file exists.** Briefs are first-class input artifacts today. Use
102	`examples/briefs/` as samples. If the user repeatedly asks from a topic, add a
103	`builtin.generate_brief` executor and call it from a topic-to-video
104	orchestrator.
105	
106	**Render is missing assets.** Rendering consumes the timeline and assets pair
107	created by cut. Do not skip cut unless both `hype.timeline.json` and
108	`hype.assets.json` already exist for that brief.
109	
110	**No one-command topic creation.** The current one-command path starts from a
111	brief file. A topic-only command should be an orchestrator that provisions the
112	brief and then delegates to the existing hype or render flow.
113	
114	## Required Formats
115	
116	All shipped content lives under packs at `astrid/packs/<pack>/`. Executor and
117	orchestrator ids must be qualified — `<pack>.<name>` — and the first segment
118	must equal the owning pack's id (e.g. `builtin.cut` lives in `packs/builtin/`,
119	`external.vibecomfy.run` lives in `packs/external/`). Element ids stay bare and
120	are scoped by `kind` (`effects`, `animations`, `transitions`).
121	
122	Executor folders use:
123	
124	```text
125	astrid/packs/<pack>/<name>/
126	  executor.yaml      # id: "<pack>.<name>"
127	  run.py
128	  STAGE.md
129	  src/               optional private helper package
130	```
131	
132	Orchestrator folders use:
133	
134	```text
135	astrid/packs/<pack>/<name>/
136	  orchestrator.yaml  # id: "<pack>.<name>"
137	  run.py
138	  STAGE.md
139	  src/               optional private helper package
140	```
141	
142	Element folders use:
143	
144	```text
145	astrid/packs/<pack>/elements/<kind>/<id>/
146	  component.tsx
147	  element.yaml       # id, kind (singular: animation|effect|transition),
148	                     # pack_id, metadata, schema, defaults, dependencies
149	```
150	
151	User-editable forks land in the gitignored `local` pack at
152	`astrid/packs/local/elements/<kind>/<id>/`. The first fork auto-creates
153	`astrid/packs/local/pack.yaml` and rewrites the copied element's `pack_id`
154	to `local`:
155	
156	```bash
157	python3 -m astrid elements fork effects text-card
158	```
159	
160	## Templates
161	
162	Copy the closest template and replace the placeholder identifiers:
163	
164	- `docs/templates/executor/`
165	- `docs/templates/orchestrator/`
166	- `docs/templates/element/`
167	
168	Then run:
169	
170	```bash
171	python3 -m astrid doctor
172	python3 -m astrid executors inspect builtin.example --json
173	python3 -m astrid orchestrators inspect builtin.example --json
174	python3 -m astrid elements inspect effects example-card --json
175	```
176	
177	Run only the inspect command that matches the thing you created.
178	
179	## Review Checklist
180	
181	- The new capability is reachable through `python3 -m astrid`.
182	- The folder has the required manifest, `run.py`, and `STAGE.md` or element
183	  files.
184	- The `STAGE.md` says when to use it and gives the canonical command.
185	- Inputs, outputs, cache behavior, isolation, dependencies, and network use are
186	  declared in metadata.
187	- Runtime outputs go under `runs/` or another ignored directory.
188	- Focused tests cover registry discovery and the behavior that can break.
189
```

35	class ExecutorRunnerError(ExecutorValidationError):
36	    """Raised when a executor cannot be prepared or executed."""

39	def _pipeline_module():
40	    from astrid.packs.builtin.hype import run as pipeline
41	
42	    return pipeline
43	
44	
45	def _builtin_steps_by_name() -> Mapping[str, Any]:
46	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
47	    steps = {step.name: step for step in pipeline.build_pool_steps()}
48	    missing = [name for name in pipeline.STEP_ORDER if name not in steps]
49	    if missing:
50	        raise ValueError(f"build_pool_steps() is missing STEP_ORDER entries: {', '.join(missing)}")
51	    return MappingProxyType(steps)
52	
53	
54	@dataclass(frozen=True)
55	class ExecutorRunRequest:
56	    executor_id: str
57	    out: Path | str
58	    project: str | None = None
59	    inputs: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
60	    outputs: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
61	    brief: Path | str | None = None
62	    dry_run: bool = False
63	    check_binaries: bool = False
64	    python_exec: str | None = None
65	    verbose: bool = False
66	    thread: str | None = None
67	    variants: int | None = None
68	    from_ref: str | None = None
69	
70	
71	@dataclass(frozen=True)
72	class ExecutorRunResult:
73	    executor_id: str
74	    kind: str
75	    command: tuple[str, ...] = ()
76	    cwd: str | None = None
77	    env: Mapping[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
78	    payload: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
79	    returncode: int | None = None
80	    dry_run: bool = False
81	    skipped: bool = False
82	    skipped_reason: str = ""
83	    missing_binaries: tuple[str, ...] = ()
84	
85	    @property
86	    def ok(self) -> bool:
87	        return not self.missing_binaries and (self.returncode is None or self.returncode == 0)
88	
89	
90	def run_executor(request: ExecutorRunRequest, registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None) -> ExecutorRunResult:
91	    if request.project and task_env.is_in_task_run(request.project):
92	        try:
93	            task_gate.gate_command(
94	                request.project,
95	                task_gate.command_for_argv(_request_argv_for_gate(request)),
96	                [],
97	                reentry=True,
98	            )
99	        except task_gate.TaskRunGateError as exc:
100	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(exc.recovery) from exc
101	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
102	    executor = active_registry.get(request.executor_id)
103	    project_context, effective_request = _prepare_project_request(request, executor)
104	    context = None if project_context is not None else thread_wrapper.begin_executor_run(effective_request, executor)
105	    try:
106	        result = _run_executor_inner(effective_request, executor)
107	    except Exception as exc:
108	        thread_wrapper.finalize_exception(context, exc)
109	        if project_context is not None:
110	            _finalize_project_executor(project_context, effective_request, status="error", returncode=-1, error=exc)
111	        raise
112	    thread_wrapper.finalize_result(context, result)
113	    if project_context is not None:
114	        _finalize_project_executor(
115	            project_context,
116	            effective_request,
117	            status=_project_status_for_result(result),
118	            returncode=result.returncode,
119	        )
120	    return result
121	
122	
123	def _request_argv_for_gate(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> tuple[str, ...]:
124	    argv = ["executors", "run", request.executor_id]
125	    if request.project:
126	        argv.extend(["--project", request.project])
127	    return tuple(argv)
128	
129	
130	def _run_executor_inner(request: ExecutorRunRequest, executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> ExecutorRunResult:
131	    if executor.id == "upload.youtube":
132	        return _run_upload_youtube(request)
133	    values = _request_values(request)
134	    _validate_required_inputs(executor, values)
135	    condition_result = evaluate_conditions(executor, values)
136	    if condition_result.skipped:
137	        return ExecutorRunResult(
138	            executor_id=executor.id,
139	            kind=executor.kind,
140	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "skipped": True, "skipped_reason": condition_result.reason},
141	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
142	            skipped=True,
143	            skipped_reason=condition_result.reason,
144	        )
145	
146	    missing_binaries = check_executor_binaries(executor) if request.check_binaries else ()
147	    if missing_binaries:
148	        return ExecutorRunResult(
149	            executor_id=executor.id,
150	            kind=executor.kind,
151	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": list(missing_binaries)},
152	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
153	            missing_binaries=missing_binaries,
154	        )
155	
156	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
157	        return _run_builtin_executor(executor, request)
158	    return _run_external_executor(executor, request, values)
159

161	def _run_upload_youtube(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> ExecutorRunResult:
162	    inputs = dict(request.inputs)
163	    if request.dry_run:
164	        return ExecutorRunResult(
165	            executor_id=request.executor_id,
166	            kind="built_in",
167	            dry_run=True,
168	            payload={"would_run": "upload.youtube", "inputs": inputs},
169	        )
170	
171	    from astrid.packs.upload.youtube.src.social_publish import publish_youtube_video
172	
173	    result = publish_youtube_video(
174	        video_url=_required_input(inputs, "video_url"),
175	        title=_required_input(inputs, "title"),
176	        description=_required_input(inputs, "description"),
177	        tags=_optional_input(inputs, "tags") or _optional_input(inputs, "tag"),
178	        privacy_status=str(_optional_input(inputs, "privacy_status") or "private"),
179	        playlist_id=_optional_input(inputs, "playlist_id"),
180	        made_for_kids=bool(_optional_input(inputs, "made_for_kids") or False),
181	    )
182	    return ExecutorRunResult(executor_id=request.executor_id, kind="built_in", payload=result)

185	@dataclass(frozen=True)
186	class ConditionResult:
187	    skipped: bool = False
188	    reason: str = ""
189	
190	
191	def evaluate_conditions(executor: ExecutorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ConditionResult:
192	    for condition in executor.conditions:
193	        result = _evaluate_condition(condition, values)
194	        if result.skipped:
195	            return result
196	    return ConditionResult()

199	def check_executor_binaries(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> tuple[str, ...]:
200	    return tuple(binary for binary in executor.isolation.binaries if shutil.which(binary) is None)

203	def build_pipeline_context(request: ExecutorRunRequest, executor: ExecutorDefinition | None = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
204	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
205	    values = _request_values(request)
206	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
207	    brief = _optional_path(values.get("brief") or request.brief)
208	    if brief is None:
209	        brief = (out / "brief.txt").resolve()
210	    audio_value = values.get("audio")
211	    video_value = values.get("video")
212	    video = _optional_asset_path(video_value)
213	    audio = _optional_asset_path(audio_value if audio_value is not None else video_value)
214	    env_file = _optional_path(values.get("env_file"))
215	    theme_raw = values.get("theme")
216	    theme_explicit = theme_raw is not None
217	    theme = pipeline._resolve_theme_arg(theme_raw) if theme_explicit else pipeline._resolve_theme_arg(pipeline.WORKSPACE_ROOT / "themes" / "banodoco-default" / "theme.json")
218	    brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief, out))
219	    brief_out = (out / "briefs" / brief_slug).resolve()
220	    skip = _as_string_list(values.get("skip"))
221	    asset_values = _as_string_list(values.get("asset") or values.get("assets"))
222	    args = argparse.Namespace(
223	        audio=audio,
224	        video=video,
225	        out=out,
226	        brief=brief,
227	        brief_out=brief_out,
228	        brief_copy=brief_out / "brief.txt",
229	        skip=skip,
230	        asset=asset_values,
231	        asset_pairs=_parse_asset_pairs(asset_values),
232	        primary_asset=values.get("primary_asset"),
233	        theme=theme,
234	        theme_explicit=theme_explicit,
235	        source_slug=str(values.get("source_slug") or out.name),
236	        brief_slug=brief_slug,
237	        env_file=env_file,
238	        extra_args=_normalize_extra_args(values.get("extra_args")),
239	        target_duration=_optional_float(values.get("target_duration")),
240	        python_exec=str(values.get("python_exec") or request.python_exec or sys.executable),
241	        render=bool(values.get("render", False)),
242	        verbose=bool(values.get("verbose", request.verbose)),
243	        no_prefetch=bool(values.get("no_prefetch", False)),
244	        keep_downloads=bool(values.get("keep_downloads", False)),
245	        cache_dir=_optional_path(values.get("cache_dir")),
246	        drift=str(values.get("drift") or "strict"),
247	        from_step=values.get("from_step"),
248	        max_editor_passes=int(values.get("max_editor_passes", 2)),
249	        editor_iteration=int(values.get("editor_iteration", 1)),
250	    )
251	    if executor is not None:
252	        args.executor_id = executor.id
253	    return args
254

256	def build_executor_command(request: ExecutorRunRequest, registry: ExecutorRegistry | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
257	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
258	    executor = active_registry.get(request.executor_id)
259	    values = _request_values(request)
260	    _validate_required_inputs(executor, values)
261	    condition_result = evaluate_conditions(executor, values)
262	    if condition_result.skipped:
263	        return ()
264	    if executor.kind == "built_in" and "pipeline_step" in executor.metadata:
265	        step = _step_for_executor(executor)
266	        args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)
267	        return tuple(step.build_cmd(args))
268	    return _expand_external_command(executor, request, values)[0]
269	
270	
271	def _run_builtin_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> ExecutorRunResult:
272	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
273	    step = _step_for_executor(executor)
274	    args = build_pipeline_context(request, executor)
275	    command = tuple(step.build_cmd(args))
276	    if request.dry_run:
277	        return ExecutorRunResult(
278	            executor_id=executor.id,
279	            kind=executor.kind,
280	            command=command,
281	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
282	            dry_run=True,
283	        )
284	    if args.brief.exists():
285	        pipeline.prepare_brief_artifacts(args)
286	    returncode = pipeline.run_step(step, list(command), args)
287	    return ExecutorRunResult(
288	        executor_id=executor.id,
289	        kind=executor.kind,
290	        command=command,
291	        payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": returncode, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
292	        returncode=returncode,
293	    )
294	
295	
296	def _run_external_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ExecutorRunResult:
297	    command, cwd, env = _expand_external_command(executor, request, values)
298	    if request.dry_run:
299	        return ExecutorRunResult(
300	            executor_id=executor.id,
301	            kind=executor.kind,
302	            command=command,
303	            cwd=cwd,
304	            env=env,
305	            payload={"executor_id": executor.id, "missing_binaries": [], "returncode": None, "skipped": False, "skipped_reason": ""},
306	            dry_run=True,
307	        )
308	    completed = subprocess.run(
309	        list(command),
310	        cwd=cwd,
311	        env={**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()},
312	        check=False,
313	    )
314	    return ExecutorRunResult(
315	        executor_id=executor.id,
316	        kind=executor.kind,
317	        command=command,
318	        cwd=cwd,
319	        env=env,
320	        payload={
321	            "executor_id": executor.id,
322	            "missing_binaries": [],
323	            "returncode": completed.returncode,
324	            "skipped": False,
325	            "skipped_reason": "",
326	        },
327	        returncode=completed.returncode,
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _expand_external_command(
332	    executor: ExecutorDefinition,
333	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
334	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
335	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
336	    if executor.command is None:
337	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} has no command")
338	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(executor, request, values)
339	    argv = tuple(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders) for part in executor.command.argv)
340	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(executor.command.cwd, placeholders) if executor.command.cwd else None
341	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in executor.command.env.items()}
342	    return argv, cwd, env
343	
344	
345	def _prepare_project_request(
346	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
347	    executor: ExecutorDefinition,
348	) -> tuple[ProjectRunContext | None, ExecutorRunRequest]:
349	    if not request.project:
350	        return None, request
351	    reject_project_with_out(request.project, request.out)
352	    context = prepare_project_run(
353	        request.project,
354	        tool_id=executor.id,
355	        kind="executor",
356	        argv=_project_argv(request),
357	        metadata={"dry_run": bool(request.dry_run)},
358	    )
359	    return context, replace(request, out=context.run_root)
360	
361	
362	def _project_argv(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> list[str]:
363	    argv = ["executors", "run", request.executor_id]
364	    if request.project:
365	        argv.extend(["--project", request.project])
366	    if request.brief:
367	        argv.extend(["--brief", str(request.brief)])
368	    for key, value in request.inputs.items():
369	        argv.extend(["--input", f"{key}={_stringify_value(value)}"])
370	    if request.dry_run:
371	        argv.append("--dry-run")
372	    if request.check_binaries:
373	        argv.append("--check-binaries")
374	    if request.python_exec:
375	        argv.extend(["--python-exec", request.python_exec])
376	    if request.verbose:
377	        argv.append("--verbose")
378	    return argv
379	
380	
381	def _project_status_for_result(result: ExecutorRunResult) -> str:
382	    if result.skipped or result.dry_run:
383	        return "skipped"
384	    if not result.ok:
385	        return "failed"
386	    return "success"
387	
388	
389	def _finalize_project_executor(
390	    context: ProjectRunContext,
391	    request: ExecutorRunRequest,
392	    *,
393	    status: str,
394	    returncode: int | None,
395	    error: BaseException | str | None = None,
396	) -> None:
397	    metadata = {"dry_run": bool(request.dry_run)}
398	    finalize_project_run(
399	        context,
400	        status=status,
401	        returncode=returncode,
402	        error=error,
403	        metadata=metadata,
404	        artifact_roots=[context.run_root],
405	    )
406	
407	
408	def _project_subprocess_env(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> dict[str, str]:
409	    return project_thread_env() if request.project else {}
410	
411	
412	def _placeholder_values(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
413	    out = Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve()
414	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
415	        "out": str(out),
416	    }
417	    python_exec = _resolve_python_exec(executor, request, values)
418	    if python_exec is not None:
419	        placeholders["python_exec"] = python_exec
420	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
421	    if brief is not None:
422	        brief_path = Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve()
423	        placeholders["brief"] = str(brief_path)
424	        brief_slug = str(values.get("brief_slug") or _default_brief_slug(brief_path, out))
425	        brief_out = out / "briefs" / brief_slug
426	        placeholders["brief_slug"] = brief_slug
427	        placeholders["brief_out"] = str(brief_out)
428	        placeholders["brief_copy"] = str(brief_out / "brief.txt")
429	    for key, value in values.items():
430	        if value is None:
431	            continue
432	        placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
433	    for output in executor.outputs:
434	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
435	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
436	        if output.placeholder:
437	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
438	    return placeholders
439	
440	
441	def _output_value(output: ExecutorOutput, request: ExecutorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
442	    if output.name in request.outputs:
443	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
444	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
445	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
446	    if output.path_template:
447	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
448	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
449	
450	
451	def _resolve_python_exec(executor: ExecutorDefinition, request: ExecutorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str | None:
452	    input_override = values.get("python_exec")
453	    if _has_value(input_override):
454	        return str(input_override)
455	    if _has_value(request.python_exec):
456	        return str(request.python_exec)
457	    if not _executor_uses_placeholder(executor, "python_exec"):
458	        return None
459	    if executor.kind == "external" and executor.isolation.mode == "subprocess":
460	        installed_python = executor_python_path(executor)
461	        if installed_python.is_file():
462	            return str(installed_python)
463	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(
464	            f"executor {executor.id!r} requires an installed Python environment; "
465	            f"run `python3 -m astrid executors install {executor.id}` or pass python_exec as an input override"
466	        )
467	    return sys.executable
468	
469	
470	def _executor_uses_placeholder(executor: ExecutorDefinition, placeholder: str) -> bool:
471	    if executor.command is None:
472	        return False
473	    needle = f"{{{placeholder}}}"
474	    if any(needle in part for part in executor.command.argv):
475	        return True
476	    if executor.command.cwd and needle in executor.command.cwd:
477	        return True
478	    return any(needle in value for value in executor.command.env.values())
479	
480	
481	def _expand_placeholders(value: str, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
482	    def replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
483	        key = match.group(1)
484	        if key not in placeholders:
485	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"missing value for placeholder {{{key}}}")
486	        return placeholders[key]
487	
488	    return _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replace, value)
489	
490	
491	def _validate_required_inputs(executor: ExecutorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
492	    missing = [
493	        port.name
494	        for port in executor.inputs
495	        if port.required and port.default is None and not _has_value(values.get(port.name))
496	    ]
497	    if missing:
498	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"executor {executor.id!r} missing required input(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
499	
500	
501	def _evaluate_condition(condition: ConditionSpec, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> ConditionResult:
502	    if condition.kind == "always":
503	        return ConditionResult()
504	    if condition.kind == "requires_input":
505	        if not condition.input or not _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
506	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires input {condition.input!r}")
507	        return ConditionResult()
508	    if condition.kind == "requires_file":
509	        candidate = values.get(condition.input) if condition.input else condition.path
510	        if not _has_value(candidate):
511	            raise ExecutorRunnerError("condition requires a file path")
512	        path = Path(str(candidate)).expanduser()
513	        if not path.is_file():
514	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"condition requires file: {path}")
515	        return ConditionResult()
516	    if condition.kind == "skip_if_input" and condition.input and _has_value(values.get(condition.input)):
517	        return ConditionResult(skipped=True, reason=f"input {condition.input!r} is set")
518	    raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"unsupported condition kind {condition.kind!r}")
519	
520	
521	def _step_for_executor(executor: ExecutorDefinition) -> Any:
522	    step_name = executor.metadata.get("pipeline_step")
523	    if not isinstance(step_name, str):
524	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} is missing metadata.pipeline_step")
525	    steps = _builtin_steps_by_name()
526	    if step_name not in steps:
527	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"built-in executor {executor.id!r} references unknown pipeline step {step_name!r}")
528	    return steps[step_name]
529	
530	
531	def _request_values(request: ExecutorRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
532	    values = dict(request.inputs)
533	    if request.brief is not None and "brief" not in values:
534	        values["brief"] = request.brief
535	    if request.python_exec is not None and "python_exec" not in values:
536	        values["python_exec"] = request.python_exec
537	    values.setdefault("verbose", request.verbose)
538	    return values
539	
540	
541	def _has_value(value: Any) -> bool:
542	    return value is not None and value != ""
543	
544	
545	def _optional_path(value: Any) -> Path | None:
546	    if value is None or value == "":
547	        return None
548	    return Path(str(value)).expanduser().resolve()
549	
550	
551	def _optional_asset_path(value: Any) -> Path | str | None:
552	    if value is None or value == "":
553	        return None
554	    text = str(value)
555	    pipeline = _pipeline_module()
556	    if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(text):
557	        return text
558	    return Path(text).expanduser().resolve()
559	
560	
561	def _optional_float(value: Any) -> float | None:
562	    if value is None or value == "":
563	        return None
564	    return float(value)
565	
566	
567	def _as_string_list(value: Any) -> list[str]:
568	    if value is None or value == "":
569	        return []
570	    if isinstance(value, str):
571	        return [value]
572	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
573	        return [str(item) for item in value]
574	    return [str(value)]
575	
576	
577	def _parse_asset_pairs(values: list[str]) -> list[tuple[str, Path | str]]:
578	    pairs: list[tuple[str, Path | str]] = []
579	    for raw in values:
580	        if "=" not in raw:
581	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
582	        key, path_text = raw.split("=", 1)
583	        key = key.strip()
584	        path_text = path_text.strip()
585	        if not key or not path_text:
586	            raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"invalid asset value {raw!r}; expected KEY=PATH")
587	        pipeline = _pipeline_module()
588	        if pipeline.asset_cache.is_url(path_text):
589	            pairs.append((key, path_text))
590	        else:
591	            pairs.append((key, Path(path_text).expanduser().resolve()))
592	    return pairs
593	
594	
595	def _normalize_extra_args(value: Any) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
596	    if value is None:
597	        return {}
598	    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
599	        raise ExecutorRunnerError("extra_args must be an object keyed by step name")
600	    return {str(key): _as_string_list(raw_values) for key, raw_values in value.items()}
601	
602	
603	def _default_brief_slug(brief: Path, out: Path) -> str:
604	    generic_brief_names = {"brief", "plan", "prompt"}
605	    return out.name if brief.stem.lower() in generic_brief_names else brief.stem
606	
607	
608	def _stringify_value(value: Any) -> str:
609	    if isinstance(value, Path):
610	        return str(value)
611	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
612	        return ",".join(str(item) for item in value)
613	    return str(value)
614	
615	
616	def _required_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> str:
617	    value = inputs.get(key)
618	    if value in (None, ""):
619	        raise ExecutorRunnerError(f"{key} is required")
620	    return str(value)
621	
622	
623	def _optional_input(inputs: Mapping[str, Any], key: str) -> Any:
624	    value = inputs.get(key)
625	    if value in (None, ""):
626	        return None
627	    return value
628	
629

133	
134	def run_orchestrator(request: OrchestratorRunRequest, registry: OrchestratorRegistry | None = None) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
135	    if request.project and task_env.is_in_task_run(request.project):
136	        try:
137	            task_gate.gate_command(
138	                request.project,
139	                task_gate.command_for_argv(_request_argv_for_gate(request)),
140	                [],
141	                reentry=True,
142	            )
143	        except task_gate.TaskRunGateError as exc:
144	            raise OrchestratorRunnerError(exc.recovery) from exc
145	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
146	    orchestrator = active_registry.get(request.orchestrator_id)
147	    project_context, effective_request = _prepare_project_request(request, orchestrator)
148	    context = None if project_context is not None else thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run(effective_request, orchestrator)
149	    try:
150	        result = _run_orchestrator_inner(effective_request, orchestrator)
151	    except Exception as exc:
152	        thread_wrapper.finalize_exception(context, exc)
153	        if project_context is not None:
154	            _finalize_project_orchestrator(project_context, effective_request, status="error", returncode=-1, error=exc)
155	        raise
156	    thread_wrapper.finalize_result(context, result)
157	    if project_context is not None:
158	        _finalize_project_orchestrator(
159	            project_context,
160	            effective_request,
161	            status=_project_status_for_result(result),
162	            returncode=result.returncode,
163	        )
164	    return result
165	
166	
167	def _request_argv_for_gate(request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> tuple[str, ...]:
168	    argv = ["orchestrators", "run", request.orchestrator_id, *request.orchestrator_args]
169	    if request.project:
170	        argv.extend(["--project", request.project])
171	    return tuple(argv)
172	
173	
174	def _run_orchestrator_inner(request: OrchestratorRunRequest, orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
175	    values = _request_values(request)
176	    _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator, request)
177	    _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator, values)
178	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "python":
179	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(_run_python_orchestrator(orchestrator, request))
180	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "command":
181	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(_run_command_orchestrator(orchestrator, request, values))
182	    raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"unsupported orchestrator runtime kind {orchestrator.runtime.kind!r}")
183	
184	
185	def build_orchestrator_command(request: OrchestratorRunRequest, registry: OrchestratorRegistry | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
186	    active_registry = registry or load_default_registry()
187	    orchestrator = active_registry.get(request.orchestrator_id)
188	    values = _request_values(request)
189	    _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator, request)
190	    _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator, values)
191	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind != "command":
192	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} does not use a command runtime")
193	    command, _, _ = _expand_command_runtime(orchestrator, request, values)
194	    return command
195	
196	
197	def _run_python_orchestrator(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
198	    runtime = orchestrator.runtime
199	    if not runtime.module or not runtime.function:
200	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} has an invalid Python runtime")
201	    try:
202	        module = importlib.import_module(runtime.module)
203	    except Exception as exc:
204	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"failed to import orchestrator runtime module {runtime.module!r}: {exc}") from exc
205	    target = getattr(module, runtime.function, None)
206	    if not callable(target):
207	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator runtime target {runtime.module}.{runtime.function} is not callable")
208	    try:
209	        raw_result = target(request, orchestrator)
210	    except OrchestratorRunnerError:
211	        raise
212	    except Exception as exc:
213	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} Python runtime failed: {exc}") from exc
214	    return _normalize_python_result(orchestrator, request, raw_result)
215	
216	
217	def _run_command_orchestrator(
218	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
219	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
220	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
221	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
222	    command, cwd, env = _expand_command_runtime(orchestrator, request, values)
223	    if request.dry_run:
224	        planned_commands = (command,)
225	        return OrchestratorRunResult(
226	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
227	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
228	            runtime_kind="command",
229	            command=command,
230	            planned_commands=planned_commands,
231	            cwd=cwd,
232	            env=env,
233	            returncode=None,
234	            dry_run=True,
235	            plan=_plan_from_commands(planned_commands, prefix=orchestrator.id),
236	        )
237	    completed = subprocess.run(
238	        list(command),
239	        cwd=cwd,
240	        env={**os.environ, **env, **_project_subprocess_env(request), **thread_wrapper.subprocess_env()},
241	        check=False,
242	    )
243	    return OrchestratorRunResult(
244	        orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
245	        kind=orchestrator.kind,
246	        runtime_kind="command",
247	        command=command,
248	        planned_commands=(command,),
249	        cwd=cwd,
250	        env=env,
251	        returncode=completed.returncode,
252	    )
253	
254	
255	def _expand_command_runtime(
256	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
257	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
258	    values: Mapping[str, Any],
259	) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], str | None, dict[str, str]]:
260	    command_spec = orchestrator.runtime.command
261	    if command_spec is None:
262	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} has no command runtime")
263	    placeholders = _placeholder_values(orchestrator, request, values)
264	    argv: list[str] = []
265	    for part in command_spec.argv:
266	        if part == "{orchestrator_args}":
267	            argv.extend(request.orchestrator_args)
268	        else:
269	            argv.append(_expand_placeholders(part, placeholders))
270	    cwd = _expand_placeholders(command_spec.cwd, placeholders) if command_spec.cwd else None
271	    env = {[REDACTED](value, placeholders) for key, value in command_spec.env.items()}
272	    return tuple(argv), cwd, env
273	
274	
275	def _normalize_python_result(
276	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
277	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
278	    raw_result: Any,
279	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
280	    if isinstance(raw_result, OrchestratorRunResult):
281	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(raw_result)
282	    if raw_result is None:
283	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
284	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
285	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
286	            runtime_kind="python",
287	            returncode=None if request.dry_run else 0,
288	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
289	        ))
290	    if isinstance(raw_result, int):
291	        return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
292	            orchestrator_id=orchestrator.id,
293	            kind=orchestrator.kind,
294	            runtime_kind="python",
295	            returncode=None if request.dry_run else raw_result,
296	            dry_run=request.dry_run,
297	        ))
298	    if isinstance(raw_result, dict):
299	        return _result_from_mapping(orchestrator, request, raw_result)
300	    raise OrchestratorRunnerError(
301	        f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} returned unsupported result type {type(raw_result).__name__}; "
302	        "expected OrchestratorRunResult, dict, int, or None"
303	    )
304	
305	
306	def _result_from_mapping(
307	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
308	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
309	    raw: Mapping[str, Any],
310	) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
311	    command = _tuple_of_strings(raw.get("command", ()), "command")
312	    planned_commands = _planned_commands(raw.get("planned_commands", (command,) if command else ()))
313	    errors = tuple(
314	        error if isinstance(error, OrchestratorRunError) else OrchestratorRunError(str(error))
315	        for error in raw.get("errors", ())
316	    )
317	    returncode = raw.get("returncode")
318	    if request.dry_run and returncode is not None:
319	        returncode = None
320	    elif returncode is not None:
321	        returncode = int(returncode)
322	    plan = _plan_from_raw(raw.get("plan")) if "plan" in raw else None
323	    return _ensure_dry_run_plan(OrchestratorRunResult(
324	        orchestrator_id=str(raw.get("orchestrator_id") or orchestrator.id),
325	        kind=str(raw.get("kind") or orchestrator.kind),
326	        runtime_kind=str(raw.get("runtime_kind") or "python"),
327	        command=command,
328	        planned_commands=planned_commands,
329	        cwd=_optional_string(raw.get("cwd")),
330	        env={str(key): str(value) for key, value in dict(raw.get("env", {})).items()},
331	        returncode=returncode,
332	        dry_run=bool(raw.get("dry_run", request.dry_run)),
333	        outputs=dict(raw.get("outputs", {})),
334	        errors=errors,
335	        plan=plan,
336	    ))
337	
338	
339	def _ensure_dry_run_plan(result: OrchestratorRunResult) -> OrchestratorRunResult:
340	    if not result.dry_run or result.plan is not None:
341	        return result
342	    return replace(result, plan=_plan_from_commands(result.planned_commands, prefix=result.orchestrator_id))
343	
344	
345	def _plan_from_commands(commands: tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...], *, prefix: str) -> OrchestratorPlan:
346	    steps = tuple(
347	        OrchestratorPlanStep(
348	            id=f"{prefix}.step_{index + 1}",
349	            kind="command",
350	            command=command,
351	        )
352	        for index, command in enumerate(commands)
353	    )
354	    return OrchestratorPlan(steps=steps)
355	
356	
357	def _plan_from_raw(raw: Any) -> OrchestratorPlan | None:
358	    if raw is None:
359	        return None
360	    if isinstance(raw, OrchestratorPlan):
361	        return raw
362	    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
363	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("plan must be an object")
364	    raw_steps = raw.get("steps", ())
365	    if not isinstance(raw_steps, (list, tuple)):
366	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("plan.steps must be a list")
367	    return OrchestratorPlan(
368	        steps=tuple(_plan_step_from_raw(item, f"plan.steps[{index}]") for index, item in enumerate(raw_steps)),
369	        summary=str(raw.get("summary") or ""),
370	    )
371	
372	
373	def _plan_step_from_raw(raw: Any, path: str) -> OrchestratorPlanStep:
374	    if isinstance(raw, OrchestratorPlanStep):
375	        return raw
376	    if not isinstance(raw, Mapping):
377	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path} must be an object")
378	    step_id = raw.get("id")
379	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id.strip():
380	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path}.id must be a non-empty string")
381	    return OrchestratorPlanStep(
382	        id=step_id,
383	        kind=str(raw.get("kind") or "command"),
384	        command=_tuple_of_strings(raw.get("command", ()), f"{path}.command"),
385	        description=str(raw.get("description") or ""),
386	        metadata=dict(raw.get("metadata", {})),
387	    )
388	
389	
390	def _placeholder_values(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, str]:
391	    placeholders: dict[str, str] = {
392	        "python_exec": str(values.get("python_exec") or request.python_exec or sys.executable),
393	        "orchestrator_args": " ".join(request.orchestrator_args),
394	        "verbose": str(bool(values.get("verbose", request.verbose))).lower(),
395	    }
396	    if request.out is not None:
397	        placeholders["out"] = str(Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve())
398	    brief = values.get("brief") or request.brief
399	    if brief is not None:
400	        placeholders["brief"] = str(Path(str(brief)).expanduser().resolve())
401	    for key, value in values.items():
402	        if value is None:
403	            continue
404	        if key == "verbose":
405	            placeholders[key] = str(bool(value)).lower()
406	        else:
407	            placeholders[key] = _stringify_value(value)
408	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
409	        output_path = _output_value(output, request, placeholders)
410	        placeholders[output.name] = output_path
411	        if output.placeholder:
412	            placeholders[output.placeholder] = output_path
413	    return placeholders
414	
415	
416	def _prepare_project_request(
417	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
418	    orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition,
419	) -> tuple[ProjectRunContext | None, OrchestratorRunRequest]:
420	    if not request.project:
421	        return None, request
422	    reject_project_with_out(request.project, request.out)
423	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind != "command":
424	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--project is currently supported only for command-runtime orchestrators")
425	    if _orchestrator_requires_output_path(orchestrator) and _has_cli_option(tuple(request.orchestrator_args), "--out"):
426	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(
427	            f"--project cannot be combined with passthrough --out for {orchestrator.id}"
428	        )
429	    context = prepare_project_run(
430	        request.project,
431	        tool_id=orchestrator.id,
432	        kind="orchestrator",
433	        argv=_project_argv(request),
434	        metadata={"dry_run": bool(request.dry_run)},
435	    )
436	    args = tuple(request.orchestrator_args)
437	    if _orchestrator_requires_output_path(orchestrator):
438	        args = (*args, "--out", str(context.run_root))
439	    return context, replace(request, out=context.run_root, orchestrator_args=args)
440	
441	
442	def _orchestrator_requires_output_path(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition) -> bool:
443	    return bool(orchestrator.metadata.get("requires_output_path"))
444	
445	
446	def _project_argv(request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> list[str]:
447	    argv = ["orchestrators", "run", request.orchestrator_id]
448	    if request.project:
449	        argv.extend(["--project", request.project])
450	    if request.brief:
451	        argv.extend(["--brief", str(request.brief)])
452	    for key, value in request.inputs.items():
453	        argv.extend(["--input", f"{key}={_stringify_value(value)}"])
454	    if request.dry_run:
455	        argv.append("--dry-run")
456	    if request.python_exec:
457	        argv.extend(["--python-exec", request.python_exec])
458	    if request.verbose:
459	        argv.append("--verbose")
460	    if request.orchestrator_args:
461	        argv.append("--")
462	        argv.extend(request.orchestrator_args)
463	    return argv
464	
465	
466	def _project_status_for_result(result: OrchestratorRunResult) -> str:
467	    if result.dry_run:
468	        return "skipped"
469	    if not result.ok:
470	        return "failed"
471	    return "success"
472	
473	
474	def _finalize_project_orchestrator(
475	    context: ProjectRunContext,
476	    request: OrchestratorRunRequest,
477	    *,
478	    status: str,
479	    returncode: int | None,
480	    error: BaseException | str | None = None,
481	) -> None:
482	    metadata = {"dry_run": bool(request.dry_run)}
483	    finalize_project_run(
484	        context,
485	        status=status,
486	        returncode=returncode,
487	        error=error,
488	        metadata=metadata,
489	        artifact_roots=[context.run_root],
490	    )
491	
492	
493	def _project_subprocess_env(request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> dict[str, str]:
494	    return project_thread_env() if request.project else {}
495	
496	
497	def _has_cli_option(args: tuple[str, ...], option: str) -> bool:
498	    return any(arg == option or arg.startswith(f"{option}=") for arg in args)
499	
500	
501	def _output_value(output: Output, request: OrchestratorRunRequest, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
502	    if output.name in request.outputs:
503	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.name])
504	    if output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs:
505	        return _stringify_value(request.outputs[output.placeholder])
506	    if output.path_template:
507	        return _expand_placeholders(output.path_template, placeholders)
508	    if request.out is None:
509	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"--out is required to derive output {output.name!r}")
510	    return str((Path(request.out).expanduser().resolve() / output.name).resolve())
511	
512	
513	def _expand_placeholders(value: str, placeholders: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
514	    def replace(match: re.Match[str]) -> str:
515	        key = match.group(1)
516	        if key not in placeholders:
517	            raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"missing value for placeholder {{{key}}}")
518	        return placeholders[key]
519	
520	    return _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replace, value)
521	
522	
523	def _validate_required_inputs(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, values: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
524	    missing = [
525	        port.name
526	        for port in orchestrator.inputs
527	        if port.required and port.default is None and not _has_value(values.get(port.name))
528	    ]
529	    if missing:
530	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"orchestrator {orchestrator.id!r} missing required input(s): {', '.join(missing)}")
531	
532	
533	def _validate_out_requirement(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> None:
534	    if request.out is not None:
535	        return
536	    if _orchestrator_requires_output_path(orchestrator):
537	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"--out is required for {orchestrator.id}")
538	    if request.dry_run:
539	        return
540	    if orchestrator.runtime.kind == "command" and _command_runtime_requires_out(orchestrator, request):
541	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--out is required for command runtime placeholders")
542	    raise OrchestratorRunnerError("--out is required for orchestrator execution")
543	
544	
545	def _command_runtime_requires_out(orchestrator: OrchestratorDefinition, request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> bool:
546	    command = orchestrator.runtime.command
547	    if command is None:
548	        return False
549	    values = [*command.argv]
550	    if command.cwd:
551	        values.append(command.cwd)
552	    values.extend(command.env.values())
553	    if any(_uses_placeholder(value, "out") for value in values):
554	        return True
555	    for output in orchestrator.outputs:
556	        if output.name in request.outputs or (output.placeholder and output.placeholder in request.outputs):
557	            continue
558	        if output.path_template is None or _uses_placeholder(output.path_template, "out"):
559	            return True
560	    return False
561	
562	
563	def _uses_placeholder(value: str, placeholder: str) -> bool:
564	    return placeholder in _PLACEHOLDER_RE.findall(value)
565	
566	
567	def _request_values(request: OrchestratorRunRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
568	    values = dict(request.inputs)
569	    if request.brief is not None and "brief" not in values:
570	        values["brief"] = request.brief
571	    if request.python_exec is not None and "python_exec" not in values:
572	        values["python_exec"] = request.python_exec
573	    values.setdefault("verbose", request.verbose)
574	    return values
575	
576	
577	def _planned_commands(raw: Any) -> tuple[tuple[str, ...], ...]:
578	    if raw is None:
579	        return ()
580	    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
581	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError("planned_commands must be a list of command lists")
582	    commands: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
583	    for index, item in enumerate(raw):
584	        commands.append(_tuple_of_strings(item, f"planned_commands[{index}]"))
585	    return tuple(commands)
586	
587	
588	def _tuple_of_strings(raw: Any, path: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
589	    if raw is None or raw == ():
590	        return ()
591	    if not isinstance(raw, (list, tuple)):
592	        raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path} must be a list")
593	    result: list[str] = []
594	    for index, item in enumerate(raw):
595	        if not isinstance(item, str):
596	            raise OrchestratorRunnerError(f"{path}[{index}] must be a string")
597	        result.append(item)
598	    return tuple(result)
599	
600	
601	def _optional_string(value: Any) -> str | None:
602	    if value is None:
603	        return None
604	    return str(value)
605	
606	
607	__all__ = [
608	    "OrchestratorRunError",
609	    "OrchestratorRunRequest",
610	    "OrchestratorRunResult",
611	    "OrchestratorRunnerError",
612	    "build_orchestrator_command",
613	    "run_orchestrator",
614	]
