1	"""DSL builders for Astrid task-mode orchestrators (Phase 4).
2	
3	Authors construct task plans in Python using the helpers exported from
4	``astrid.orchestrate``. The DSL emits a JSON payload byte-shape-equivalent
5	to the schema accepted by ``astrid.core.task.plan.load_plan``.
6	
7	Construction-time guards are intentional: the typo trap on
8	``step.<missing_produces>``, sentinel-only attested rejection, the
9	``orchestrators run`` argv guard, reserved-attribute collisions, and
10	duplicate sibling-id detection all raise ``OrchestrateDefinitionError`` at
11	definition time so authoring errors surface immediately.
12	"""
13	
14	from __future__ import annotations
15	
16	import json
17	import os
18	import shlex
19	import tempfile
20	from dataclasses import dataclass
21	from typing import Any, Callable, Optional, Tuple, Union
22	
23	from astrid.core.task.plan import (
24	    TaskPlanError,
25	    _strip_astrid_prefix,
26	    load_plan,
27	)
28	from astrid.verify import Check, canonical_check_params
29	
30	
31	class OrchestrateDefinitionError(Exception):
32	    """Raised when an orchestrator DSL definition is invalid."""
33	
34	
35	_RESERVED_ATTRS = frozenset(
36	    {"id", "kind", "command", "argv", "plan", "produces", "repeat", "instructions", "ack"}
37	)
38	_VALID_UNTIL = frozenset({"user_approves", "verifier_passes", "quorum"})
39	_VALID_ON_EXHAUST = frozenset({"escalate", "fail"})
40	_VALID_ACK_KINDS = frozenset({"agent", "actor"})
41	
42	
43	@dataclass(frozen=True)
44	class _ProducesHandle:
45	    step_id: str
46	    name: str
47	
48	    def __str__(self) -> str:
49	        return f"{self.step_id}.produces.{self.name}"
50	
51	
52	@dataclass(frozen=True)
53	class _RepeatUntilSpec:
54	    condition: str
55	    max_iterations: int
56	    on_exhaust: str
57	    quorum_n: Optional[int] = None
58	
59	
60	@dataclass(frozen=True)
61	class _RepeatForEachSpec:
62	    items: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]]
63	    from_ref: Any  # str | _ProducesHandle | None
64	
65	
66	_RepeatSpec = Union[_RepeatUntilSpec, _RepeatForEachSpec]
67	
68	
69	class _StepHandle:
70	    """A typed reference to a declared step.
71	
72	    Attribute access on an unknown name first looks up declared produces and
73	    returns a ``_ProducesHandle``; misses raise ``OrchestrateDefinitionError``
74	    so authoring typos fail at definition time rather than at runtime.
75	    """
76	
77	    def __init__(
78	        self,
79	        *,
80	        id: str,
81	        kind: str,
82	        command: Optional[str] = None,
83	        argv: Optional[Tuple[str, ...]] = None,
84	        plan: Any = None,
85	        produces: Optional[dict] = None,
86	        repeat: Optional[_RepeatSpec] = None,
87	        instructions: Optional[str] = None,
88	        ack: Optional[dict] = None,
89	        cost_hint_usd: Optional[float] = None,
90	    ) -> None:
91	        self.id = id
92	        self.kind = kind
93	        self.command = command
94	        self.argv = argv
95	        self.plan = plan
96	        self.produces = produces or {}
97	        self.repeat = repeat
98	        self.instructions = instructions
99	        self.ack = ack
100	        self.cost_hint_usd = cost_hint_usd
101	
102	    def __getattr__(self, name: str) -> _ProducesHandle:
103	        produces = self.__dict__.get("produces", {})
104	        if name in produces:
105	            return _ProducesHandle(self.__dict__.get("id", "<unknown>"), name)
106	        step_id = self.__dict__.get("id", "<unknown>")
107	        declared = ", ".join(sorted(produces.keys())) if produces else "<none>"
108	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
109	            f"step {step_id!r} has no produces {name!r}; declared produces: [{declared}]"
110	        )
111	
112	    def __repr__(self) -> str:
113	        return f"_StepHandle(id={self.id!r}, kind={self.kind!r})"
114	
115	
116	class _PlanBuilder:
117	    """A tree of steps; emits a load_plan-compatible JSON payload."""
118	
119	    def __init__(self, plan_id: str, steps: list) -> None:
120	        if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:
121	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError("plan_id must be a non-empty string")
122	        if not isinstance(steps, list):
123	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
124	                f"plan {plan_id!r} steps must be a list, got {type(steps).__name__}"
125	            )
126	        seen: set = set()
127	        for index, step in enumerate(steps):
128	            if not isinstance(step, _StepHandle):
129	                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
130	                    f"plan {plan_id!r} steps[{index}] must be a _StepHandle, "
131	                    f"got {type(step).__name__}"
132	                )
133	            if step.id in seen:
134	                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
135	                    f"plan {plan_id!r} has duplicate sibling step id {step.id!r}"
136	                )
137	            seen.add(step.id)
138	        self.plan_id = plan_id
139	        self.steps: Tuple[_StepHandle, ...] = tuple(steps)
140	
141	    def _build_payload(
142	        self,
143	        *,
144	        _resolver: Optional[Callable[..., "_PlanBuilder"]] = None,
145	        _visiting: Optional[set] = None,
146	    ) -> dict:
147	        if _visiting is None:
148	            _visiting = set()
149	        if self.plan_id in _visiting:
150	            chain = " -> ".join(sorted(_visiting) + [self.plan_id])
151	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
152	                f"nested cycle: plan {self.plan_id!r} re-entered (chain: {chain})"
153	            )
154	        next_visiting = _visiting | {self.plan_id}
155	        return {
156	            "plan_id": self.plan_id,
157	            "version": 1,
158	            "steps": [
159	                _step_to_dict(step, _resolver=_resolver, _visiting=next_visiting)
160	                for step in self.steps
161	            ],
162	        }
163	
164	    def to_dict(
165	        self,
166	        *,
167	        _resolver: Optional[Callable[..., "_PlanBuilder"]] = None,
168	        _visiting: Optional[set] = None,
169	    ) -> dict:
170	        payload = self._build_payload(_resolver=_resolver, _visiting=_visiting)
171	        # Round-trip through the public load_plan validator (FLAG-002).
172	        fp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
173	            "w", suffix=".json", delete=False, encoding="utf-8"
174	        )
175	        try:
176	            json.dump(payload, fp)
177	            fp.flush()
178	            tmp_path = fp.name
179	        finally:
180	            fp.close()
181	        try:
182	            load_plan(tmp_path)
183	        except TaskPlanError as exc:
184	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
185	                f"DSL output for plan {self.plan_id!r} failed validation: {exc}"
186	            ) from exc
187	        finally:
188	            try:
189	                os.unlink(tmp_path)
190	            except OSError:
191	                pass
192	        return payload
193	
194	    def __repr__(self) -> str:
195	        return f"_PlanBuilder(plan_id={self.plan_id!r}, steps={len(self.steps)})"
196	
197	
198	def _step_to_dict(
199	    step: _StepHandle,
200	    *,
201	    _resolver: Optional[Callable[..., _PlanBuilder]],
202	    _visiting: set,
203	) -> dict:
204	    if step.kind == "code":
205	        out: dict = {"id": step.id, "kind": "code", "command": step.command}
206	    elif step.kind == "attested":
207	        out = {
208	            "id": step.id,
209	            "kind": "attested",
210	            "command": step.command,
211	            "instructions": step.instructions,
212	            "ack": dict(step.ack or {}),
213	        }
214	    elif step.kind == "nested":
215	        nested_plan = step.plan
216	        if isinstance(nested_plan, _PlanBuilder):
217	            child_payload = nested_plan._build_payload(
218	                _resolver=_resolver, _visiting=_visiting
219	            )
220	        elif isinstance(nested_plan, str):
221	            if _resolver is None:
222	                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
223	                    f"nested step {step.id!r} uses string plan ref {nested_plan!r}; "
224	                    "compile via astrid.orchestrate.compile.compile_to_path "
225	                    "or pass _resolver= to to_dict()"
226	                )
227	            sub_builder = _resolver(nested_plan, _visiting=_visiting)
228	            if not isinstance(sub_builder, _PlanBuilder):
229	                raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
230	                    f"nested step {step.id!r} resolver returned "
231	                    f"{type(sub_builder).__name__}, expected _PlanBuilder"
232	                )
233	            child_payload = sub_builder._build_payload(
234	                _resolver=_resolver, _visiting=_visiting
235	            )
236	        else:
237	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
238	                f"nested step {step.id!r} plan must be _PlanBuilder or "
239	                f"qualified id string, got {type(nested_plan).__name__}"
240	            )
241	        out = {"id": step.id, "kind": "nested", "plan": child_payload}
242	    else:
243	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
244	            f"step {step.id!r} has unknown kind {step.kind!r}"
245	        )
246	
247	    if step.produces:
248	        out["produces"] = _produces_to_dict(step.produces)
249	    if step.repeat is not None:
250	        out["repeat"] = _repeat_to_dict(step.repeat)
251	    return out
252	
253	
254	def _produces_to_dict(produces: dict) -> dict:
255	    out: dict = {}
256	    for name in sorted(produces.keys()):
257	        path, check = produces[name]
258	        out[name] = {
259	            "path": path,
260	            "check": {
261	                "check_id": check.check_id,
262	                "params": canonical_check_params(check.params),
263	                "sentinel": check.sentinel,
264	            },
265	        }
266	    return out
267	
268	
269	def _repeat_to_dict(repeat: _RepeatSpec) -> dict:
270	    if isinstance(repeat, _RepeatUntilSpec):
271	        out: dict = {
272	            "until": repeat.condition,
273	            "max_iterations": repeat.max_iterations,
274	            "on_exhaust": repeat.on_exhaust,
275	        }
276	        if repeat.quorum_n is not None:
277	            out["quorum_n"] = repeat.quorum_n
278	        return out
279	    if isinstance(repeat, _RepeatForEachSpec):
280	        if repeat.items is not None:
281	            return {"for_each": {"items": list(repeat.items)}}
282	        return {"for_each": {"from": str(repeat.from_ref)}}
283	    raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
284	        f"unknown repeat spec type: {type(repeat).__name__}"
285	    )
286	
287	
288	def _normalize_produces(raw: Any, step_id: str, *, kind: str) -> dict:
289	    if raw is None:
290	        return {}
291	    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
292	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
293	            f"step {step_id!r} produces must be a dict, got {type(raw).__name__}"
294	        )
295	    out: dict = {}
296	    for name, value in raw.items():
297	        if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
298	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
299	                f"step {step_id!r} produces names must be non-empty strings"
300	            )
301	        if name in _RESERVED_ATTRS:
302	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
303	                f"step {step_id!r} produces name {name!r} collides with reserved "
304	                f"_StepHandle attribute; choose a different name"
305	            )
306	        check, path = _coerce_produces_value(value, step_id, name)
307	        if kind == "attested" and check.sentinel:
308	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
309	                f"attested step {step_id!r} produces[{name!r}] uses sentinel-only "
310	                f"check {check.check_id!r}; supply a semantic check (e.g. "
311	                f"json_file(), audio_duration_min(...), image_dimensions(...))"
312	            )
313	        out[name] = (path, check)
314	    return out
315	
316	
317	def _coerce_produces_value(value: Any, step_id: str, name: str) -> Tuple[Check, str]:
318	    """Accept a bare ``Check`` (path defaults to the produces name) or a
319	    ``(check, path)`` tuple. Anything else raises.
320	    """
321	    if isinstance(value, Check):
322	        return value, name
323	    if isinstance(value, tuple) and len(value) == 2:
324	        a, b = value
325	        if isinstance(a, Check) and isinstance(b, str) and b:
326	            return a, b
327	        if isinstance(b, Check) and isinstance(a, str) and a:
328	            return b, a
329	    raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
330	        f"step {step_id!r} produces[{name!r}] must be a Check or (check, path) "
331	        f"tuple, got {type(value).__name__}"
332	    )
333	
334	
335	def _normalize_argv(argv: Any, step_id: str) -> Tuple[str, ...]:
336	    if not isinstance(argv, list) or not argv:
337	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
338	            f"code step {step_id!r} argv must be a non-empty list"
339	        )
340	    out: list = []
341	    for index, element in enumerate(argv):
342	        if isinstance(element, _ProducesHandle):
343	            out.append(str(element))
344	        elif isinstance(element, str):
345	            out.append(element)
346	        else:
347	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
348	                f"code step {step_id!r} argv[{index}] must be str or "
349	                f"_ProducesHandle, got {type(element).__name__}"
350	            )
351	    return tuple(out)
352	
353	
354	def _reject_orchestrators_run_argv(argv: Tuple[str, ...], step_id: str) -> None:
355	    tokens = _strip_astrid_prefix(list(argv))
356	    if len(tokens) >= 2 and tokens[0] == "orchestrators" and tokens[1] == "run":
357	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
358	            f"code step {step_id!r} argv targets 'astrid orchestrators run'; "
359	            "use a nested(plan=...) step instead"
360	        )
361	
362	
363	def _normalize_repeat(repeat: Any, step_id: str) -> Optional[_RepeatSpec]:
364	    if repeat is None:
365	        return None
366	    if not isinstance(repeat, (_RepeatUntilSpec, _RepeatForEachSpec)):
367	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
368	            f"step {step_id!r} repeat must come from repeat_until() or "
369	            f"repeat_for_each(), got {type(repeat).__name__}"
370	        )
371	    return repeat
372	
373	
374	def _normalize_ack(ack: Any, step_id: str) -> dict:
375	    if isinstance(ack, str):
376	        kind = ack
377	    elif isinstance(ack, dict) and "kind" in ack:
378	        kind = ack["kind"]
379	    else:
380	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
381	            f"attested step {step_id!r} ack must be 'agent', 'actor', or "
382	            f"a dict with 'kind'; got {type(ack).__name__}"
383	        )
384	    if kind not in _VALID_ACK_KINDS:
385	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
386	            f"attested step {step_id!r} ack.kind must be one of "
387	            f"{sorted(_VALID_ACK_KINDS)}, got {kind!r}"
388	        )
389	    return {"kind": kind}
390	
391	
392	def code(
393	    step_id: str,
394	    *,
395	    argv: list,
396	    produces: Optional[dict] = None,
397	    repeat: Optional[_RepeatSpec] = None,
398	    cost_hint_usd: Optional[float] = None,
399	) -> _StepHandle:
400	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
401	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError("code step_id must be a non-empty string")
402	    normalized_argv = _normalize_argv(argv, step_id)
403	    _reject_orchestrators_run_argv(normalized_argv, step_id)
404	    command = shlex.join(normalized_argv)
405	    produces_dict = _normalize_produces(produces, step_id, kind="code")
406	    repeat_spec = _normalize_repeat(repeat, step_id)
407	    return _StepHandle(
408	        id=step_id,
409	        kind="code",
410	        command=command,
411	        argv=normalized_argv,
412	        produces=produces_dict,
413	        repeat=repeat_spec,
414	        cost_hint_usd=cost_hint_usd,
415	    )
416	
417	
418	def attested(
419	    step_id: str,
420	    *,
421	    command: str,
422	    instructions: str,
423	    ack: Any,
424	    produces: Optional[dict] = None,
425	    repeat: Optional[_RepeatSpec] = None,
426	    cost_hint_usd: Optional[float] = None,
427	) -> _StepHandle:
428	    if not isinstance(step_id, str) or not step_id:
429	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError("attested step_id must be a non-empty string")
430	    if not isinstance(command, str) or not command:
431	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
432	            f"attested step {step_id!r} command must be a non-empty string"
433	        )
434	    if not isinstance(instructions, str) or not instructions:
435	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
436	            f"attested step {step_id!r} instructions must be a non-empty string"
437	        )
438	    ack_dict = _normalize_ack(ack, step_id)
439	    produces_dict = _normalize_produces(produces, step_id, kind="attested")
440	    repeat_spec = _normalize_repeat(repeat, step_id)
441	    return _StepHandle(
442	        id=step_id,
443	        kind="attested",
444	        command=command,
445	        instructions=instructions,
446	        ack=ack_dict,
447	        produces=produces_dict,
448	        repeat=repeat_spec,
449	        cost_hint_usd=cost_hint_usd,
450	    )
451	
452	
453	def nested(
454	    step_id: str,
455	    *,
456	    plan: Any,
457	    produces: Optional[dict] = None,
458	    repeat: Optional[_RepeatSpec] = None,
459	    cost_hint_usd: Optional[float] = None,
460	) -> _StepHandle:

462	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError("nested step_id must be a non-empty string")
463	    if not isinstance(plan, (_PlanBuilder, str)):
464	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
465	            f"nested step {step_id!r} plan must be a _PlanBuilder or qualified "
466	            f"id string, got {type(plan).__name__}"
467	        )
468	    if isinstance(plan, str) and not plan:
469	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
470	            f"nested step {step_id!r} plan string must be non-empty"
471	        )
472	    produces_dict = _normalize_produces(produces, step_id, kind="nested")
473	    repeat_spec = _normalize_repeat(repeat, step_id)
474	    return _StepHandle(
475	        id=step_id,
476	        kind="nested",
477	        plan=plan,
478	        produces=produces_dict,
479	        repeat=repeat_spec,
480	        cost_hint_usd=cost_hint_usd,
481	    )
482	
483	
484	def repeat_until(
485	    condition: str,
486	    *,
487	    max_iterations: int,
488	    on_exhaust: str,
489	    quorum_n: Optional[int] = None,
490	) -> _RepeatUntilSpec:
491	    if condition not in _VALID_UNTIL:
492	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
493	            f"repeat_until condition must be one of {sorted(_VALID_UNTIL)}, "
494	            f"got {condition!r}"
495	        )
496	    if not isinstance(max_iterations, int) or isinstance(max_iterations, bool) or max_iterations < 1:
497	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
498	            f"repeat_until max_iterations must be an int >= 1, got {max_iterations!r}"
499	        )
500	    if on_exhaust not in _VALID_ON_EXHAUST:
501	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
502	            f"repeat_until on_exhaust must be one of {sorted(_VALID_ON_EXHAUST)}, "
503	            f"got {on_exhaust!r}"
504	        )
505	    if condition == "quorum":
506	        if not isinstance(quorum_n, int) or isinstance(quorum_n, bool) or quorum_n < 1:
507	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
508	                "repeat_until quorum_n must be an int >= 1 when condition='quorum'"
509	            )
510	    elif quorum_n is not None:
511	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
512	            "repeat_until quorum_n is only valid when condition='quorum'"
513	        )
514	    return _RepeatUntilSpec(
515	        condition=condition,
516	        max_iterations=max_iterations,
517	        on_exhaust=on_exhaust,
518	        quorum_n=quorum_n,
519	    )
520	
521	
522	def repeat_for_each(
523	    items: Optional[list] = None,
524	    *,
525	    from_: Any = None,
526	) -> _RepeatForEachSpec:
527	    if (items is None) == (from_ is None):
528	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
529	            "repeat_for_each requires exactly one of items= or from_="
530	        )
531	    if items is not None:
532	        if not isinstance(items, list) or not items or not all(
533	            isinstance(x, str) and x for x in items
534	        ):
535	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
536	                "repeat_for_each items must be a non-empty list of non-empty strings"
537	            )
538	        return _RepeatForEachSpec(items=tuple(items), from_ref=None)
539	    if not isinstance(from_, (_ProducesHandle, str)) or (
540	        isinstance(from_, str) and not from_
541	    ):
542	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
543	            "repeat_for_each from_ must be a non-empty string or _ProducesHandle"
544	        )
545	    return _RepeatForEachSpec(items=None, from_ref=from_)
546	
547	
548	def plan(plan_id: str, steps: list) -> _PlanBuilder:
549	    return _PlanBuilder(plan_id, steps)
550	
551	
552	def orchestrator(plan_id: str) -> Callable[[Any], _PlanBuilder]:
553	    if not isinstance(plan_id, str) or not plan_id:
554	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError("orchestrator plan_id must be a non-empty string")
555	
556	    def _decorator(target: Any) -> _PlanBuilder:
557	        if isinstance(target, _PlanBuilder):
558	            return target
559	        if isinstance(target, list):
560	            return _PlanBuilder(plan_id, target)
561	        if callable(target):
562	            result = target()
563	            if isinstance(result, _PlanBuilder):
564	                return result
565	            if isinstance(result, list):
566	                return _PlanBuilder(plan_id, result)
567	            raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
568	                f"@orchestrator({plan_id!r}) target returned "
569	                f"{type(result).__name__}; expected _PlanBuilder or list of steps"
570	            )
571	        raise OrchestrateDefinitionError(
572	            f"@orchestrator({plan_id!r}) decorated object must be a callable, "
573	            f"_PlanBuilder, or list, got {type(target).__name__}"
574	        )
575	
576	    return _decorator
577

1	"""Inline produces-check primitives.
2	
3	Each factory returns a frozen ``Check`` whose ``run(path)`` produces a
4	``CheckResult``. Checks are stdlib-only — ``wave`` for WAV duration,
5	``ffprobe`` subprocess fallback for non-WAV audio, raw PNG/JPEG header
6	parsing for image_dimensions.
7	
8	``sentinel`` flags whether a check is sentinel-only (existence-only).
9	``file_nonempty`` is sentinel-only; the rest are semantic. ``all_of`` is
10	sentinel iff every constituent is sentinel.
11	"""
12	
13	from __future__ import annotations
14	
15	import json
16	import subprocess
17	import wave
18	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
19	from pathlib import Path
20	from typing import Any, Callable
21	
22	
23	@dataclass(frozen=True)
24	class CheckResult:
25	    ok: bool
26	    reason: str = ""
27	    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
28	
29	
30	_RUNNER_REGISTRY: dict[str, Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]] = {}
31	
32	
33	@dataclass(frozen=True)
34	class Check:
35	    check_id: str
36	    params: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
37	    sentinel: bool = False
38	
39	    def run(self, path: Path) -> CheckResult:
40	        runner = _RUNNER_REGISTRY.get(self.check_id)
41	        if runner is None:
42	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"unknown check_id: {self.check_id}")
43	        return runner(path, self.params)
44	
45	
46	def canonical_check_params(params: Any) -> Any:
47	    if isinstance(params, dict):
48	        return {k: canonical_check_params(params[k]) for k in sorted(params)}
49	    if isinstance(params, (list, tuple)):
50	        return [canonical_check_params(item) for item in params]
51	    return params
52	
53	
54	def _register(check_id: str) -> Callable[[Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]], Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]]:
55	    def decorator(fn: Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]) -> Callable[[Path, dict[str, Any]], CheckResult]:
56	        _RUNNER_REGISTRY[check_id] = fn
57	        return fn
58	    return decorator
59	
60	
61	def file_nonempty() -> Check:
62	    return Check(check_id="file_nonempty", params={}, sentinel=True)
63	
64	
65	@_register("file_nonempty")
66	def _run_file_nonempty(path: Path, _params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
67	    if not path.exists():
68	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
69	    try:
70	        size = path.stat().st_size
71	    except OSError as exc:
72	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"stat failed: {exc}")
73	    if size <= 0:
74	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file is empty: {path}", details={"size": size})
75	    return CheckResult(ok=True, details={"size": size})
76	
77	
78	def json_file() -> Check:
79	    return Check(check_id="json_file", params={}, sentinel=False)
80	
81	
82	@_register("json_file")
83	def _run_json_file(path: Path, _params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
84	    try:
85	        raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
86	    except FileNotFoundError:
87	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
88	    except OSError as exc:
89	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"read failed: {exc}")
90	    try:
91	        json.loads(raw)
92	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
93	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"invalid JSON: {exc.msg}")
94	    return CheckResult(ok=True)
95	
96	
97	def json_schema(schema: dict[str, Any]) -> Check:
98	    return Check(check_id="json_schema", params={"schema": canonical_check_params(schema)}, sentinel=False)
99	
100	
101	@_register("json_schema")
102	def _run_json_schema(path: Path, params: dict[str, Any]) -> CheckResult:
103	    try:
104	        payload = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
105	    except FileNotFoundError:
106	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"file does not exist: {path}")
107	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
108	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"invalid JSON: {exc.msg}")
109	    except OSError as exc:
110	        return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"read failed: {exc}")
111	    schema = params.get("schema") or {}
112	    required = schema.get("required") or []
113	    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
114	        if required:
115	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason="payload is not an object")
116	        return CheckResult(ok=True)
117	    for key in required:
118	        if key not in payload:
119	            return CheckResult(ok=False, reason=f"missing required key: {key}")
120	    return CheckResult(ok=True)

2	
3	Phase 2 attested/nested handling is kernel-only; Phase 5 will add the

17	Status = str
18	
19	
20	@dataclass(frozen=True)
21	class DoctorCheck:
22	    name: str
23	    status: Status
24	    detail: str
25	    required: bool = True
26	
27	    def failed(self, *, strict_optional: bool = False) -> bool:
28	        if self.status == "fail":
29	            return True
30	        return strict_optional and not self.required and self.status == "warn"
31	
32	
33	def run_checks(*, optional_binaries: tuple[str, ...] = ("ffmpeg", "npx", "uv", "npm")) -> tuple[DoctorCheck, ...]:
34	    checks: list[DoctorCheck] = []
35	    checks.append(_check_python_version())
36	    checks.append(_check_required_imports())
37	    executor_registry = _capture_check("executor registry", _check_executor_registry)
38	    checks.append(executor_registry)
39	    checks.append(_capture_check("orchestrator registry", _check_orchestrator_registry))
40	    checks.append(_capture_check("element registry", _check_element_registry))
41	    checks.append(_capture_check("repo structure", _check_repo_structure))
42	    checks.append(_capture_check("vibecomfy metadata", _check_vibecomfy_metadata))
43	    checks.append(_capture_check("remotion config", _check_remotion_config))
44	    checks.append(_capture_check("timeline catalog", _check_timeline_catalog))
45	    checks.append(_check_projects_root())
46	    for binary in optional_binaries:
47	        checks.append(_check_optional_binary(binary))
48	    return tuple(checks)
49	
50	
51	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
52	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python3 -m astrid doctor", description="Check the Astrid environment.")
53	    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Emit machine-readable diagnostics.")
54	    parser.add_argument(
55	        "--strict-optional",
56	        action="store_true",
57	        help="Treat missing optional external binaries as failures.",
58	    )
59	    return parser

10	def _runpod_lifecycle_root() -> Path:
11	    configured = getattr(sys, "_vibecomfy_runpod_lifecycle_root", None)
12	    if configured:
13	        return Path(configured)
14	    configured_env = os.getenv("VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT")
15	    if configured_env:
16	        return Path(configured_env)
17	    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3] / "runpod-lifecycle"
18	
19	
20	def _runpod_lifecycle_main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
21	    try:
22	        from dotenv import load_dotenv
23	        from runpod_lifecycle.cli import main as runpod_main
24	    except ImportError:
25	        root = _runpod_lifecycle_root()
26	        src = root / "src"
27	        if not src.exists():
28	            print(
29	                "runpod-lifecycle is not installed. Install VibeComfy with `pip install -e '.[runpod-local]'` "
30	                "or set VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT for a local checkout.",
31	                file=sys.stderr,
32	            )
33	            return 1
34	        sys.path.insert(0, str(src))
35	        try:
36	            from dotenv import load_dotenv
37	            from runpod_lifecycle.cli import main as runpod_main
38	        except Exception as exc:
39	            print(f"could not import runpod-lifecycle: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
40	            return 1
41	    root = _runpod_lifecycle_root()
42	    load_dotenv(root / ".env")
43	    return runpod_main(argv)
44	
45

50	    if args.json:
51	        argv.append("--json")
52	    return _runpod_lifecycle_main(argv)
53	
54

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	# VibeComfy Implementation Plan
2	
3	VibeComfy should become a Python-first way to discover, patch, compile, and run ComfyUI workflows from official templates and installed custom-node examples. The first milestone is not a perfect workflow-to-Python translator. The first milestone is a reliable template loader, inspector, patcher, scratchpad generator, and runner that can execute real image and video workflows locally and on RunPod.
4	
5	## High-Level Summary
6	
7	We are building a pure-Python scratchpad for ComfyUI workflows. It should pull real workflows from the official Comfy workflow-template packages and from installed custom-node `example_workflows`, convert them into a VibeComfy internal format, let users edit and extend them with ordinary Python, then run them end to end through ComfyUI without opening the UI.
8	
9	The proof is execution, not just conversion. The first milestone must inspect at least 20 real workflow examples and run at least five representative workflows end to end, including official templates, video/Wan workflows, and at least one external custom-node workflow from a source such as `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. The RunPod validation path must launch a GPU machine, run the workflows, save outputs and metadata, and terminate the machine when finished.
10	
11	The central artifact is a scratchpad file: a generated Python file for one workflow instantiation. It starts from a full workflow template, applies prompt/model/seed edits, optionally composes individual nodes from the installed node library, compiles to a Comfy workflow dict, and queues that dict in the embedded/pip ComfyUI runtime.
12	
13	RunPod is part of the core validation plan, not an optional final polish step. Local tests can prove parsing, conversion, and small runtime behavior, but real confidence requires launching a GPU pod, installing the full stack, running multiple workflows end to end, and verifying cleanup. This document currently lives in the same repository as the `runpod-lifecycle` launcher, so agents executing the plan should use the launcher in this directory for remote validation.
14	
15	## Source-Informed Adjustments
16	
17	- The official `Comfy-Org/workflow_templates` repository is structured around packages, manifests, templates, blueprints, validation scripts, model metadata, and optional node-version metadata. Treat it as a structured source of truth, not as a folder of arbitrary JSON files.
18	- Comfy custom-node repositories can expose workflow examples through `example_workflows/` and related folder names. VibeComfy should scan installed custom nodes for these examples instead of only vendoring hand-picked external JSON files.
19	- ComfyScript already provides a Python front end and workflow-to-Python transpiler. Run a spike before deciding whether VibeComfy should reuse it, wrap it, import from it, export to it, or only learn from it.
20	- Use the exact external source names during implementation. For custom-node validation, start with `kijai/ComfyUI-KJNodes` and `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`, not vague `Kijai/Kajai` placeholders.
21	- The node library should be runtime-discovered from installed Comfy node definitions or node mappings. Do not hard-code an "approved node" list as the primary source of truth.
22	- GraphBuilder should be verified locally before making it a required backend. If direct API-dict generation is simpler and more reliable for the first milestone, use it while keeping GraphBuilder as an optional compiler backend.
23	- ComfyUI has two distinct workflow JSON shapes: the UI graph (with `nodes`, `links`, positions, groups — what the web UI exports) and the API dict (a flat object keyed by stringified node id — what `queue_prompt` accepts). Templates in `Comfy-Org/workflow_templates` are mostly UI exports; custom-node `example_workflows/` mix both. `load_template` must detect which shape it received and route accordingly; `normalize_to_api` is a real conversion, not a passthrough. Patching the wrong shape is the most common silent failure.
24	
25	## Operating Principles
26	
27	- Keep moving. The agent should not wait for feedback when there is a reasonable next action. If a dependency is missing, inspect it, install it, stub around it, or choose the smallest reversible path that keeps progress moving.
28	- Bias toward simple, working paths. Prefer a boring JSON-to-internal-format translator plus a pure-Python scratchpad over a clever compiler that blocks end-to-end execution.
29	- Solve the whole chain. A workflow is not validated until it runs end to end, writes outputs, saves metadata, and can be reproduced from a clean command.
30	- Make hidden state visible. Models, custom nodes, storage mounts, environment variables, and external workflow sources must be reported clearly in inspect output, logs, and docs.
31	- Treat failures as implementation tasks. Missing models, missing custom nodes, bad workflow shapes, queue errors, and remote setup failures should produce fixes or explicit follow-up tasks, not vague blockers.
32	- Use official templates as the main validation corpus. Pull most workflows from the official Comfy template library, then add a smaller custom-node slice from installed `example_workflows` to prove compatibility.
33	- Clean up paid resources. Any RunPod machine launched for validation must be terminated at the end of the run unless the user explicitly asks to leave it running.
34	
35	## Non-Negotiables
36	
37	- VibeComfy is a pure-Python scratchpad layer. Users should be able to write Python that starts from templates, converts them into VibeComfy's internal format, edits that representation, adds custom Python logic, and runs the result.
38	- The agent-facing unit of work is a normal Python scratchpad file. Agents should inspect, edit, validate, run, and repair code, not perform fragile ad hoc JSON mutation.
39	- JSON passthrough is allowed only as a source and compatibility path. Any editing, patching, or composition must happen through `VibeWorkflow`, then compile back to a Comfy API dict.
40	- The scratchpad path must run end to end. It is not enough to inspect or export workflows; the generated workflow must execute through Comfy and produce real outputs.
41	- Internal and external code must work together. The test plan must validate workflows from the official template library, workflows that require custom nodes, and Python code that programmatically modifies those workflows.
42	- The first implementation can be pragmatic. JSON passthrough and a simple internal IR are acceptable if they preserve behavior and unlock reliable patching and execution.
43	- At least five representative workflows must run end to end before the milestone is considered credible.
44

192	## 3. Define the Core VibeWorkflow and Scratchpad Model
193	
194	Do this before implementing `convert_to_vibe_format`. The converter needs a target type, and the scratchpad needs one meaning.
195	
196	### VibeWorkflow v0
197	
198	`VibeWorkflow` is the internal editable representation of one Comfy workflow. It is not raw Comfy JSON and it is not a generated Python file.
199	
200	Minimum data model:
201	
202	```python
203	@dataclass
204	class VibeWorkflow:
205	    id: str
206	    source: WorkflowSource
207	    nodes: dict[str, VibeNode]
208	    edges: list[VibeEdge]
209	    inputs: dict[str, VibeInput]
210	    outputs: list[VibeOutput]
211	    requirements: WorkflowRequirements
212	    metadata: dict[str, Any]
213	
214	@dataclass
215	class VibeNode:
216	    id: str
217	    class_type: str
218	    pack: str | None
219	    inputs: dict[str, Any]
220	    widgets: dict[str, Any]
221	    metadata: dict[str, Any]
222	
223	@dataclass
224	class VibeEdge:
225	    from_node: str
226	    from_output: str
227	    to_node: str
228	    to_input: str
229	```
230	
231	Minimum methods:
232	
233	```python
234	workflow.set_prompt("...")
235	workflow.set_seed(42)
236	workflow.set_steps(20)
237	workflow.set_model("...")
238	workflow.set_input("width", 1024)
239	workflow.add_node("KJNodes.SomeNode", **inputs)
240	workflow.connect("node.output", "other.input")
241	workflow.compile() -> ComfyApiWorkflowDict
242	workflow.validate() -> ValidationReport
243	```
244	
245	Rules:
246	
247	- Keep node ids stable during conversion so diffs and error reports are understandable.
248	- Preserve unknown node fields in `metadata` so round-tripping does not destroy information.
249	- All edits go through `VibeWorkflow` methods or typed helper functions.
250	- Raw JSON passthrough means "load or execute unmodified source JSON," not "mutate JSON dictionaries by hand."
251	
252	Knob mapping:
253	
254	`set_prompt`, `set_seed`, `set_steps`, `set_model`, and `set_input` are sugar over a per-template knob map that resolves a name to a concrete `(node_id, field_path)` pair. The knob map is built during `convert_to_vibe_format` from heuristics over the indexed node classes:
255	
256	- `prompt` (alias `positive_prompt`) -> first `CLIPTextEncode` node whose downstream is the positive conditioning input of the sampler.
257	- `negative_prompt` -> the negative-side `CLIPTextEncode`.
258	- `seed` -> the `seed` widget on the primary `KSampler` / `KSamplerAdvanced`.
259	- `steps`, `cfg`, `sampler_name`, `scheduler` -> matching widgets on the same sampler.
260	- `model` -> the `ckpt_name` / `unet_name` widget on the loader feeding the sampler.
261	- `width`, `height` -> the matching widgets on `EmptyLatentImage` or equivalent.
262	
263	When heuristics are ambiguous (multiple samplers, multiple text encoders), `set_*` raises rather than guessing. Templates may ship an explicit `<template>.knobs.toml` sidecar that overrides the heuristic map; this is the preferred long-term path.
264	
265	### Scratchpad File
266	
267	A scratchpad is a generated Python file that builds and runs one `VibeWorkflow`. It is the agent-facing artifact. There should not also be a separate public `Scratchpad("wan")` object API in milestone one.
268	
269	Canonical scratchpad shape:
270	
271	```python
272	from vibecomfy import workflow_from_template, run
273	
274	
275	def build():
276	    workflow = workflow_from_template("flux_txt2img")
277	    workflow.set_prompt("a cinematic robot painter")
278	    workflow.set_seed(123)
279	    workflow.set_steps(20)
280	    return workflow
281	
282	
283	async def main():
284	    result = await run(build())
285	    print(result.outputs)
286	```
287	
288	Agents edit the file. The runtime imports the file, calls `build()`, validates the returned `VibeWorkflow`, compiles it to a Comfy API dict, and queues it.
289	
290	Deliverables:
291	
292	- `docs/vibeworkflow.md`, one page that specifies the v0 model.
293	- `docs/scratchpad_file.md`, one page that specifies generated scratchpad file shape.
294	- `vibecomfy/workflow.py`
295	- `vibecomfy/scratchpad_loader.py`
296	
297	Acceptance check:
298	
299	- `convert_to_vibe_format(api_workflow) -> VibeWorkflow` has a concrete target type.
300	- `vibecomfy convert <template-id> --out out/scratchpads/foo.py` generates the canonical file shape.
301	- `vibecomfy validate out/scratchpads/foo.py` imports the file and validates the returned `VibeWorkflow` without running it.

465	## 6. Validate ComfyScript and Compiler Options
466	
467	ComfyScript is not automatically the VibeComfy core abstraction, but it is too relevant to ignore. It has a Python workflow front end and a workflow-to-Python transpiler, so evaluate it after representative workflows have been discovered and normalized.
468	
469	Spike commands:
470	
471	```bash
472	python -m comfy_script.transpile <workflow.json> --api http://127.0.0.1:8188/
473	uvx --from "comfy-script[default]" python -m comfy_script.transpile <workflow.json> --api http://127.0.0.1:8188/
474	```
475	
476	Spike sample:
477	
478	- One simple official SD/SDXL workflow.
479	- One FLUX workflow.
480	- One image-to-image or inpaint workflow.
481	- One Wan/video workflow.
482	- One external workflow from `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`.
483	
484	Questions to answer:
485	
486	- Does ComfyScript transpilation preserve graph behavior across representative workflows?
487	- Is the generated Python agent-editable, or too opaque?
488	- Can custom-node workflows transpile cleanly?
489	- Can ComfyScript code be used as an import/export adapter without owning VibeComfy's internal model?
490	- Does the ComfyScript runtime compete with, duplicate, or simplify the planned scratchpad runner?
491	
492	Decision options:
493	
494	- `reuse`: build VibeComfy scratchpads directly on top of ComfyScript primitives.
495	- `wrap`: use ComfyScript for import/export but keep VibeComfy's own scratchpad API.
496	- `learn`: copy lessons from the transpiler but do not depend on it.
497	- `defer`: skip ComfyScript for milestone one because it blocks simpler end-to-end execution.
498	
499	Deliverables:
500	
501	- `docs/comfyscript_spike.md`
502	- `vibecomfy/adapters/comfyscript.py`, only if the spike justifies it.
503	- Execution note: completed with decision `learn`. ComfyScript requires a live Comfy server and only transpiled `api_flux2` in the five-sample spike. It failed on official UI workflows containing `MarkdownNote` and on the Wan external sample because `MiDaS-DepthMapPreprocessor` was not registered in the active runtime. This makes it useful as a reference/possible adapter, not the milestone-one core.
504	
505	Acceptance check:
506	
507	- The document records a clear decision with evidence from at least five workflow attempts.
508	- The spike can run on workflows selected by sections 4 and 5.
509	- The main plan is updated so ComfyScript is either a real adapter path or explicitly deferred.
510	
511	## 7. Specify Comfy Server Lifecycle and Async Execution
512	
513	Nothing can run until the Comfy execution model is explicit. Milestone one should support a managed local server by default, with an option to connect to an already-running server.
514	
515	Runtime modes:
516	
517	- `embedded`: VibeComfy uses HiddenSwitch's in-process `Comfy` client, calls `queue_prompt_api`, waits for completion, and records output metadata. This is the CLI default for end-to-end local smoke runs because HTTP `/prompt` only proves queue submission.
518	- `managed`: VibeComfy starts `comfyui serve` as a subprocess, waits for readiness, queries `/object_info` or queues workflows, captures logs, and shuts it down when the run completes.
519	- `external`: VibeComfy connects to an existing Comfy server URL such as `http://127.0.0.1:8188`.
520	
521	Default for CLI:
522	
523	```text
524	embedded runtime unless --runtime server is provided
525	```
526	
527	Default for Python:
528	
529	```python
530	await run(workflow)  # uses managed runtime unless configured otherwise
531	await run(workflow, server_url="http://127.0.0.1:8188")  # uses external runtime
532	```
533	
534	Async bridge:
535	
536	- Python API remains async: `await run(workflow)`.
537	- CLI uses `asyncio.run(...)` internally.
538	- Scratchpad files may define `build()` only; the CLI handles async execution.
539	- Scratchpad files may optionally define `async main()` for direct script execution, but `vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/foo.py` only requires `build()`.
540	
541	Server lifecycle requirements:
542	
543	- Start managed server with explicit model/custom-node/output paths from `RuntimeProfile`.
544	- Poll readiness before queueing.
545	- Capture stdout/stderr to run logs.
546	- Shut down on success, validation failure, runtime exception, and keyboard interrupt.
547	- If using `external`, never shut down the user's server.
548	
549	Deliverables:
550	
551	- `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py`
552	- `vibecomfy/runtime/profile.py`
553	- `docs/runtime_lifecycle.md`
554	
555	Acceptance check:
556	
557	```bash
558	vibecomfy runtime doctor
559	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/foo.py
560	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/foo.py --server-url http://127.0.0.1:8188
561	```
562	
563	Pass condition:
564	
565	- Managed mode can start, use, log, and stop Comfy.
566	- External mode can use an already-running server and leave it running.
567	- CLI commands invoke async execution safely through `asyncio.run`.
568	
569	## 8. Build the Scratchpad and JSON Runner First
570	
571	The fastest useful product is a reliable runner for generated Python scratchpads, existing Comfy workflow JSON, and VibeComfy's internal workflow format. Build this before attempting beautiful Python export.
572	
573	Execution path:
574	
575	```text
576	template JSON -> normalized API dict -> VibeWorkflow -> scratchpad file -> queue_prompt -> outputs
577	```
578	
579	The scratchpad file is generated from a source workflow. It is the agent-facing artifact to inspect, edit, validate, run, and repair.
580	
581	Target API:
582	
583	```python
584	from vibecomfy import workflow_from_template, run
585	
586	workflow = workflow_from_template("flux_txt2img")
587	workflow.set_prompt("a cinematic robot painter")
588	workflow.set_steps(20)
589	workflow.set_seed(123)
590	
591	outputs = await run(workflow)
592	```
593	
594	CLI targets:
595	
596	```bash
597	vibecomfy convert <template-id> --out out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py
598	vibecomfy validate out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py
599	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py
600	vibecomfy run workflow.json
601	```
602	
603	Runtime output requirements:
604	
605	- Output files saved to a predictable directory.
606	- Metadata saved with prompt, seed, template id, workflow hash, and git SHA.
607	- Runtime logs captured per run.
608	- Queue errors surfaced with the relevant missing model or node name.
609	- Runs saved under `out/runs/<run-id>/`.
610	
611	Deliverables:
612	
613	- `vibecomfy/templates/template.py`
614	- `vibecomfy/runtime/convert.py`
615	- `vibecomfy/runtime/client.py`
616	- `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`
617	- `vibecomfy/scratchpad_loader.py`
618	- `vibecomfy convert`
619	- `vibecomfy validate`
620	- `vibecomfy run`
621	
622	Acceptance check:
623	
624	Run at least five representative workflows end to end and confirm output files exist:
625	
626	- One SD or SDXL text-to-image workflow from the official template library.
627	- One FLUX text-to-image workflow from the official template library.
628	- One image-to-image or inpaint workflow from the official template library.
629	- One Wan/video workflow.
630	- One external workflow from `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`.
631	
632	## 9. Add Optional GraphBuilder Backend
633	
634	Build this as a second compile backend after scratchpad execution and direct API-dict compilation work. The first version should prioritize lossless round-tripping and executable output over elegant generated code. GraphBuilder is useful only if the runtime spike proves it is available and reliable in the pip/embedded ComfyUI environment.
635	
636	Compiler paths:
637	
638	```text
639	A. Direct API dict:
640	   template JSON -> normalized API dict -> queue_prompt
641	
642	B. Scratchpad direct compile:
643	   scratchpad Python -> VibeWorkflow -> API dict -> queue_prompt
644	
645	C. GraphBuilder backend:
646	   template JSON -> intermediate graph IR -> GraphBuilder Python function -> workflow dict -> queue_prompt
647	```
648	
649	Target API:
650	
651	```python
652	workflow = workflow_from_template("flux_txt2img")
653	workflow.set_prompt("a cinematic robot painter")
654	workflow.set_steps(20)
655	workflow.set_seed(123)
656	api_dict = workflow.compile(backend="graphbuilder")
657	```
658	
659	Support custom code on top:
660	
661	```python
662	def make_variations(base_template, prompts):
663	    for prompt in prompts:
664	        workflow = workflow_from_template(base_template)
665	        workflow.set_prompt(prompt)
666	        yield workflow
667	```
668	
669	CLI target:
670	
671	```bash
672	vibecomfy convert <template-id> --out out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py
673	vibecomfy validate out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py --backend graphbuilder
674	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/<template-id>.py --backend graphbuilder
675	```
676	
677	Deliverables:
678	
679	- `vibecomfy/graphbuilder/ir.py`
680	- `vibecomfy/graphbuilder/compiler.py`
681	- `vibecomfy/adapters/api_dict.py`
682	- Execution note: completed as a pragmatic optional backend inside `VibeWorkflow.compile("graphbuilder")` using `comfy_execution.graph_utils.GraphBuilder`. Tests confirm GraphBuilder output matches the direct API-dict backend for the current graph model, and `api_flux2` compiles identically through both backends.
683	
684	Acceptance check:
685	
686	- Convert at least five templates to scratchpad files.
687	- The generated scratchpads can rebuild workflow dicts accepted by `queue_prompt`.
688	- At least five generated Python scratchpad files execute successfully.
689	- GraphBuilder outputs execute successfully if the GraphBuilder spike confirms support for the selected node shapes.
690	
691	Risk note:
692	
693	- Do not block the project on perfectly translating every Comfy workflow into hand-crafted Python or GraphBuilder. Make scratchpad execution correct first, then improve compiler backends template by template.
694	
695	## 10. Design the Agent-Facing CLI
696	
697	The scratchpad file is the user-facing and agent-facing Python layer for starting from a known template, converting it into VibeComfy's internal format, modifying it with code, and running it end to end.
698	
699	Canonical scratchpad file:
700	
701	```python
702	from vibecomfy import workflow_from_template, run
703	
704	
705	def build():
706	    workflow = workflow_from_template("wan_txt2video")
707	    workflow.set_prompt("a floating crystal city at sunrise")
708	    workflow.set_model("wan2.1")
709	    workflow.add_node("KJNodes.SomeNode", ...)
710	    workflow.connect(...)
711	    return workflow
712	
713	
714	async def main():
715	    result = await run(build())
716	    print(result.outputs)
717	```
718	
719	Minimum CLI commands:
720	
721	```bash
722	vibecomfy sources sync
723	vibecomfy workflows list
724	vibecomfy nodes list
725	vibecomfy inspect <template-id>
726	vibecomfy convert <template-id> --out out/scratchpads/foo.py
727	vibecomfy validate out/scratchpads/foo.py
728	vibecomfy doctor out/scratchpads/foo.py
729	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/foo.py
730	vibecomfy logs <run-id>
731	vibecomfy smoke
732	```
733	
734	Agent command design:
735	
736	- `sources sync`: fetch or refresh official workflow templates, installed custom-node examples, and optional vendored external examples.
737	- `workflows list/inspect`: answer what full workflows exist and what they require.
738	- `nodes list/inspect`: answer what individual nodes exist and what inputs/outputs they expose.
739	- `convert`: generate an editable scratchpad file from a workflow.
740	- `validate`: compile without running and report missing models, nodes, or invalid connections.
741	- `run`: execute a scratchpad or workflow JSON and save outputs.
742	- `doctor`: explain what broke and suggest the next concrete fix. It must separate Python import/build errors in user scratchpad code from workflow validation errors, missing model/node errors, and Comfy runtime errors.
743	- `logs`: show run logs and output metadata.
744	
745	Deliverables:
746	
747	- Public exports from `vibecomfy/__init__.py`
748	- CLI command coverage in tests.
749	- `docs/agent_interface.md`
750	- `docs/errors_and_doctor.md`, a one-page spec for structured errors and doctor output.
751	
752	Acceptance check:
753	
754	- A user can start from a template, convert it to VibeComfy's internal format, change prompt and seed in a generated Python file, and run it without opening the ComfyUI UI.
755	- A user can add external Python control flow around internal VibeComfy workflow edits and execute a batch.
756	- An agent can use CLI commands to discover workflows, discover nodes, generate a scratchpad, validate it, run it, and diagnose failures without manual UI interaction.
757	
758	## 11. Install and Verify Custom Node Packs
759	
760	Video workflows will likely require custom nodes. Treat custom-node installation as a documented, reproducible step rather than hidden state.
761	
762	Initial node packs:
763	
764	- ComfyUI core
765	- ComfyScript
766	- `kijai/ComfyUI-KJNodes`
767	- `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`
768	- ComfyUI-Manager, optional
769	
770	Installer checklist:
771	
772	```bash
773	mkdir -p custom_nodes
774	cd custom_nodes
775	git clone https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-KJNodes.git
776	git clone https://github.com/kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper.git
777	git -C ComfyUI-KJNodes checkout <pinned-sha>
778	git -C ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper checkout <pinned-sha>
779	cd ..
780	uv pip install --no-deps -r custom_nodes/<node>/requirements.txt
781	uv pip check
782	```
783	
784	Install custom-node `requirements.txt` with `--no-deps` first, then run `uv pip check` and resolve conflicts manually. Letting `uv pip` follow transitive dependencies will silently downgrade or reinstall `torch` and break the embedded ComfyUI runtime — Kijai's repos in particular pin specific torch nightlies that disagree with whatever HiddenSwitch installed.
785	
786	Verification:
787	
788	```bash
789	comfyui env check
790	comfyui --guess-settings
791	vibecomfy inspect <wan-template-id>
792	```
793	
794	Custom-node validation should go beyond installation. Exercise a small set of node-pack-specific behavior:
795	
796	- Inspect node classes and confirm they appear in Comfy's registered node mappings.
797	- Load at least one workflow that uses each required node pack.
798	- Run at least one external workflow that mixes custom nodes with core Comfy nodes.
799	- Scan `example_workflows/` and related folders from installed custom-node repositories.
800	- Confirm errors identify the missing node pack, not just the raw missing class name.
801	
802	Deliverables:
803	
804	- `docs/custom_nodes.md`
805	- Optional helper script: `scripts/install_custom_nodes.py`
806	- `scripts/verify_custom_nodes.py`
807	- Requirement detection that says exactly which custom node pack is missing.
808	
809	Acceptance check:
810	
811	- A fresh environment can install the documented node packs and inspect a Wan video workflow without unresolved custom-node errors.
812	- At least one custom-node workflow runs end to end.
813	- External custom-node code and internal VibeComfy Python code are used together in the same validation run.

815	## 12. Prove the RunPod Live Path
816	
817	Use `runpod-lifecycle` to launch a GPU pod, attach the desired network storage volume, install VibeComfy, and run real workflows remotely.
818	
819	Known working launcher context:
820	
821	- This plan file is in the same repository/directory as the `runpod-lifecycle` launcher.
822	- Package path: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/runpod-lifecycle`
823	- Python command currently needs Python 3.10+.
824	- For this user's account, `Peter` storage volume resolved to `m6ccu1lodp` in the live smoke run. This must be configuration, not a hard-coded default.
825	- A previous smoke launch successfully reached SSH and ran `nvidia-smi` on an RTX 4090.
826	- Model files are expected on the attached storage volume at standard ComfyUI paths (`models/checkpoints/`, `models/loras/`, `models/vae/`, `models/clip/`, `models/diffusion_models/`, `models/text_encoders/`). The `Peter` volume already contains the milestone-1 weight set; documenting fresh-volume bootstrap (which weights, which URLs, which hashes, which auth) is deferred to milestone 2.
827	
828	Local launch flow:
829	
830	```python
831	import asyncio
832	import time
833	
834	from dotenv import load_dotenv
835	from runpod_lifecycle import RunPodConfig, launch
836	
837	
838	async def main():
839	    load_dotenv()
840	    config = RunPodConfig.from_env(
841	        storage_name="Peter",
842	        storage_volumes=(),
843	        gpu_type="NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090",
844	        ram_tiers=(32, 16),
845	    )
846	    pod = await launch(config, name=f"vibecomfy-{int(time.time())}")
847	    try:
848	        await pod.wait_ready(timeout=300)
849	        code, stdout, stderr = await pod.exec_ssh("nvidia-smi -L", timeout=60)
850	        assert code == 0, stderr
851	        print(pod.id)
852	        print(stdout)
853	        # Continue with remote install and workflow execution.
854	    finally:
855	        await pod.terminate()
856	
857	
858	asyncio.run(main())
859	```
860	
861	Implementation note: the real validation launcher should wrap this pattern in a context manager with signal handling and a watchdog. RunPod's current pod docs expose explicit stop/delete calls and a scheduled local stop pattern; network-volume pods should be terminated rather than stopped. The pod should only be left running when the user explicitly asks for that.
862	
863	Remote setup flow:
864	
865	```bash
866	git clone <vibecomfy repo>
867	cd vibecomfy
868	uv venv --python 3.12
869	source .venv/bin/activate
870	uv pip install -e .
871	uv pip install --torch-backend=auto "comfyui@git+https://github.com/hiddenswitch/ComfyUI.git"
872	comfyui env check
873	```
874	
875	Remote smoke commands:
876	
877	```bash
878	vibecomfy smoke
879	vibecomfy sources sync
880	vibecomfy workflows list
881	vibecomfy convert <sdxl-template> --out out/scratchpads/sdxl.py
882	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/sdxl.py
883	vibecomfy convert <flux-template> --out out/scratchpads/flux.py
884	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/flux.py
885	vibecomfy convert <wan-template> --out out/scratchpads/wan.py
886	vibecomfy run out/scratchpads/wan.py
887	```
888	
889	Deliverables:
890	
891	- `docs/runpod.md`
892	- `vibecomfy smoke`
893	- One reproducible command or script for a fresh RunPod smoke test.
894	- A launch context manager that records the pod id and terminates it on normal exit, exception, or signal.
895	- A cleanup command that terminates the launched pod by id.
896	- A watchdog backstop when RunPod does not expose a first-class pod TTL through the launcher, so an abandoned validation pod does not run indefinitely.
897	
898	Acceptance check:
899	
900	- One fresh RunPod launch can install VibeComfy, run at least five representative workflows, save outputs, and terminate cleanly at the end of the run.
901	- The final test script terminates the machine it launched unless the user explicitly requested that it stay running.
902	- A forced failure during validation still runs cleanup.
903

Follow-up media validation synced 473 official template JSON files from the official Comfy template library, generated five model-backed official PNG outputs, generated one KJNodes custom-node PNG output, generated one WEBM video output, and terminated the validation pod. Fresh final validation on `4pz5727nh80qe2` repeated the proper matrix and additionally generated a real official Wan model-backed `text_to_video_wan` MP4 through both baseline Comfy and VibeComfy before terminating the pod.

1069	- [x] Runtime-generated node index includes Comfy core nodes and installed custom nodes.
1070	- [x] `VibeWorkflow` v0 is specified in one page and implemented as the only editable workflow IR.
1071	- [x] Scratchpad is consistently defined as a generated Python file with `build()`, not a second public object API.
1072	- [x] Generated scratchpad files live under `out/scratchpads/`; package modules do not use the same path for user-generated files.
1073	- [x] Comfy server lifecycle is specified and implemented for managed and external runtime modes.
1074	- [x] CLI sync-to-async bridge is specified and uses `asyncio.run` or equivalent safely.
1075	- [x] ComfyScript spike completed with an explicit reuse/wrap/learn/defer decision.
1076	- [x] At least 20 templates inspect cleanly.
1077	- [x] At least 20 official or external workflow examples are collected and inspect cleanly.
1078	- [x] At least 15 inspected examples come from the official Comfy template library.
1079	- [x] At least 5 representative workflows run end to end through VibeComfy's internal workflow format. Current status: five official model-backed image workflows, one KJNodes custom-node workflow, one WEBM video workflow, and one official Wan model-backed MP4 generated outputs on RunPod.
1080	- [x] At least 5 editable Python scratchpad files are generated and validated.
1081	- [x] At least 5 templates convert to executable Python scratchpad files.
1082	- [x] GraphBuilder is either validated as a backend or explicitly deferred in favor of direct API-dict generation.
1083	- [x] At least 1 Wan/video workflow runs on RunPod. Current status: official `text_to_video_wan` generated a real model-backed MP4 on RunPod through both baseline Comfy and VibeComfy.
1084	- [x] At least 1 external custom-node workflow runs end to end.
1085	- [x] Pure-Python scratchpad code can modify and run workflows without UI interaction.
1086	- [x] External custom-node workflows and internal VibeComfy Python edits are validated together.
1087	- [x] `kijai/ComfyUI-KJNodes` and `kijai/ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper` installation is documented.
1088	- [x] Custom-node installs are pinned by commit SHA in the install script or lockfile.
1089	- [x] Structured error types are specified for missing model, missing node, invalid graph, runtime failure, and insufficient VRAM/device profile.
1090	- [x] Missing model and missing custom-node errors are readable.
1091	- [x] Outputs are saved with prompt, seed/template inputs, template id, workflow hash, and git SHA.
1092	- [x] One command can reproduce a smoke test on fresh RunPod and terminate the launched machine at the end.
1093	- [x] RunPod cleanup is protected by context manager, signal handling, and TTL/watchdog where supported. Current status: context manager, signal handling, explicit termination, final pod listing, and a `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_MAX_RUNTIME_SECONDS` watchdog are implemented; RunPod's current pod docs expose explicit stop/delete and local scheduled stop patterns rather than a first-class pod TTL through this launcher.
1094	
1095	## Sense Check
1096	
1097	The main risk is trying to make every workflow export as beautiful Python immediately. That should be treated as a later quality pass. The first useful product is a dependable template loader, converter, patcher, runner, scratchpad, and requirements reporter.
1098	
1099	The second risk is hiding environment state. Custom nodes, models, and RunPod storage need to be explicit in docs and command output, otherwise a workflow may work once on one machine and fail everywhere else.
1100	
1101	The third risk is under-validating by running only one happy-path workflow. The plan now requires at least five end-to-end workflow runs and at least 20 inspected examples across official and external sources.
1102	
1103	The fourth risk is overfitting to raw JSON. JSON passthrough is useful for bootstrapping, but the product goal is a pure-Python scratchpad over VibeComfy's internal workflow format.
1104	
1105	The fifth risk is assuming the architecture before testing the real tools. ComfyScript, GraphBuilder, runtime node definitions, official template manifests, and custom-node `example_workflows` must be inspected directly before their role is locked in.
1106	
1107	The sixth risk is silent success. ComfyUI has two distinct workflow JSON shapes (UI vs. API), `queue_prompt` will return success for a workflow that produced a black image or dropped a connection, and a literal executor will check boxes ("output file exists", "no errors") on broken work. Every end-to-end run needs a normalize-then-validate gate plus an output-sanity check; every CLI command needs a smoke test that proves it does the work, not just that it returns 0.
1108
```

1	---
2	name: "astrid"
3	short_description: "Astrid — file-based toolkit for agents to make video, image, and audio art alongside a human."
4	description: "Use for the Astrid repo: a file-based toolkit for agents to make art and creative work alongside a human. Video edits, generative timelines, image/audio/video understanding and generation — all behind one CLI gateway."
5	---
6	
7	# Astrid
8	
9	A file-based toolkit for agents to make art and creative work alongside a human.
10	
11	Three kinds of beings live here:
12	
13	- **Executors** — run one concrete unit of work
14	- **Orchestrators** — coordinate executors (and other orchestrators) into workflows
15	- **Elements** — reusable render building blocks (effects, animations, transitions)
16	
17	`python3 -m astrid` is the executable package gateway. Every summons passes through this one gate.
18	
19	## First commands
20	
21	Run from the repository root:
22	
23	```bash
24	git status --short
25	python3 -m astrid --help
26	python3 -m astrid doctor
27	python3 -m astrid orchestrators list
28	python3 -m astrid executors list
29	python3 -m astrid elements list
30	python3 -m astrid setup
31	```
32	
33	`setup` is dry-run by default; pass `--apply` to mutate.
34	
35	## Using tools
36	
37	Find an id:
38	
39	```bash
40	python3 -m astrid [executors|orchestrators|elements] list
41	python3 -m astrid [executors|orchestrators|elements] search <terms>
42	```
43	
44	If you don't know which tool to use, run `python3 -m astrid <kind> search <terms>` first — don't guess from id alone.
45	
46	Inspect to see inputs, outputs, and intent:
47	
48	```bash
49	python3 -m astrid [executors|orchestrators|elements] inspect <id> --json
50	```
51	
52	Run it:
53	
54	```bash
55	python3 -m astrid [executors|orchestrators] run <id> -- <args>
56	```
57	
58	Each tool has its own `STAGE.md` next to its `run.py`. That is the source of truth — read it before invoking. The JSON inspect output points at the folder root and `stage_file`; load only the one relevant `STAGE.md`, not all of them.
59	
60	At the start of any session that will produce runs, run python3 -m astrid thread show @active first. The [thread] prefix on every command output is your continuous indicator; if it shows the wrong thread, run thread new or pass --thread @new to your next command. Selections are append-only; the most recent write is authoritative on read; prior selections are preserved as history but do not affect current keepers.
61	
62	Before rendering an iteration video, run `python3 -m astrid.packs.builtin.iteration_video.run inspect <thread>` to see modalities, renderers, quality, cache counts, and estimated cost without rendering.
63	
64	<!-- BEGIN CAPABILITY INDEX (auto-generated by scripts/gen_capability_index.py) -->
65	
66	### Executors
67	
68	| id | short_description |
69	| --- | --- |
70	| `builtin.arrange` | Compose a brief-specific shot arrangement from the source clip pool. |
71	| `builtin.asset_cache` | Manage the repo-local hype asset cache (download, prune, list). |
72	| `builtin.audio_understand` | Inspect audio clips or sampled windows with an audio-understanding LLM. |
73	| `builtin.boundary_candidates` | Package candidate video frames for visual scene-boundary review. |
74	| `builtin.cut` | Build the Reigh-compatible hype timeline + assets + metadata JSON triple from arrangement. |
75	| `builtin.editor_review` | Run heuristic editorial reviewers over an arrangement and emit notes. |
76	| `builtin.foley_review` | Build a static review.html pairing each tile clip with its generated Foley audio for sense-checking. |
77	| `builtin.generate_image` | Generate image files with OpenAI GPT Image models from a prompt file. |
78	| `builtin.html_canvas_effect` | Scaffold a local Remotion HTML-in-canvas effect element. |
79	| `builtin.human_notes` | Convert human editorial notes into structured pipeline inputs. |
80	| `builtin.inspect_cut` | Inspect a generated cut run directory and report timeline/asset health. |
81	| `builtin.open_in_reigh` | Copy or stage generated timeline+assets for handoff into a Reigh project. |
82	| `builtin.pool_build` | Build the candidate clip pool from triaged source-video scenes. |
83	| `builtin.pool_merge` | Merge multiple candidate clip pools into a unified pool for arrangement. |
84	| `builtin.publish` | Publish a finished timeline + assets pair into a Reigh project via API. |
85	| `builtin.quality_zones` | Tag arrangement clips with per-zone quality grades for downstream picks. |
86	| `builtin.quote_scout` | Scan a transcript for quotable lines suitable for hype clips. |
87	| `builtin.refine` | Apply targeted reviewer-driven refinements to an existing arrangement. |
88	| `builtin.reigh_data` | Fetch canonical Reigh project data through the reigh-data Edge Function. |
89	| `builtin.render` | Render a hype timeline to hype.mp4 through the Remotion compositor. |
90	| `builtin.scene_describe` | Caption each detected scene with a vision model for downstream selection. |
91	| `builtin.scenes` | Detect source-video scene boundaries with ffmpeg-driven analysis. |
92	| `builtin.shots` | Slice scenes into shot windows for downstream pool building. |
93	| `builtin.spatial_audio_page` | Build a static page that mixes Foley tracks anchored to spatial rectangles via Web Audio. |
94	| `builtin.sprite_sheet` | Generate, slice, and preview GPT Image sprite sheets for batch image work. |
95	| `builtin.tile_video` | Crop a video into an MxN grid of overlapping spatial tiles plus first-frame PNGs. |
96	| `builtin.transcribe` | Transcribe source audio to transcript.json via Whisper. |
97	| `builtin.triage` | Triage source-video scenes by quality before pool building. |
98	| `builtin.understand` | Dispatch to the audio, visual, or video understanding executor based on --mode. |
99	| `builtin.validate` | Validate the rendered video against its declared timeline and metadata. |
100	| `builtin.video_understand` | Inspect synchronized audio+video windows with a video-understanding model. |
